\|[{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{t}-{\boldsymbol{\beta}}^{*}]_{{\mathcal{S}}^{*}\setminus{\mathcal{S}}_{1}}\|=\|[{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{0}-{\boldsymbol{\beta}}^{*}]_{{\mathcal{S}}^{*}\setminus{\mathcal{S}}_{1}}\|.

For entries on
{\mathcal{S}}_{1}\setminus{\mathcal{S}}^{*}
, using a similar analysis to the proof of Lemma
1
, we have

\|[{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{t+1}-{\boldsymbol{\beta}}^{*}]_{{\mathcal{S}}_{1}}\|^{2}\leq\left(1-\eta_{t}\lambda_{\min}\right)\left\|\left[{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{t}-{\boldsymbol{\beta}}^{*}\right]_{{\mathcal{S}}_{1}}\right\|^{2}+2\eta_{t}^{2}\lambda_{\max}s\sigma^{2}\\
+\eta_{t}\|[{\boldsymbol{\beta}}^{*}]_{{\mathcal{S}}^{*}\setminus{\mathcal{S}}_{1}}\|^{2}\cdot\frac{(\lambda_{\max,s}^{\textsf{Off}})^{2}}{\lambda_{\min}}-2\eta_{t}\boldsymbol{\varXi}_{1}^{(t)}+2\eta_{t}\boldsymbol{\varXi}_{2}^{(t)}+\eta_{t}^{2}\boldsymbol{\varXi}_{3}^{(t)},

where
\boldsymbol{\varXi}_{1}^{(t)},\boldsymbol{\varXi}_{2}^{(t)},\boldsymbol{\varXi}_{3}^{(t)}
are inherited from the the proof of Lemma
1
with the substitution of
{\mathcal{S}}_{1}
in
{\mathcal{S}}_{0}
accordingly. We accumulate the above equation from
t=\tau_{1}
and it arrives at

\begin{split}&\|[{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{t+1}-{\boldsymbol{\beta}}^{*}]_{{\mathcal{S}}_{1}}\|^{2}\leq\prod_{j=\tau_{1}}^{t}\left(1-\frac{C_{a}}{j+C_{b}s\log(2d/s)}\right)\left\|\left[{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{\tau_{1}}-{\boldsymbol{\beta}}^{*}\right]_{{\mathcal{S}}_{1}}\right\|^{2}\\
&~~~~~+2s\sigma^{2}C_{a}^{2}\frac{\lambda_{\max}}{\lambda_{\min}^{2}}\sum_{l=\tau_{1}}^{t}\prod_{j=l}^{t}\left(1-\frac{C_{a}}{j+C_{b}s\log(2d/s)}\right)\left(\frac{1}{l+C_{b}s\log(2d/s)}\right)^{2}\\
&~~~~~+C_{a}\sum_{l=\tau_{1}}^{t}\prod_{j=l}^{t}\left(1-\frac{C_{a}}{j+C_{b}s\log(2d/s)}\right)\frac{1}{l+C_{b}s\log(2d/s)}\|[{\boldsymbol{\beta}}^{*}]_{{\mathcal{S}}^{*}\setminus{\mathcal{S}}_{1}}\|^{2}\cdot\frac{(\lambda_{\max,s}^{\textsf{Off}})^{2}}{\lambda_{\min}^{2}}\\
&~~~~~-2\frac{C_{a}}{\lambda_{\min}}\sum_{l=\tau_{1}}^{t}\prod_{j=l}^{t}\left(1-\frac{C_{a}}{j+C_{b}s\log(2d/s)}\right)\frac{1}{l+C_{b}s\log(2d/s)}\boldsymbol{\varXi}_{1}^{(l)}\\
&~~~~~+2\frac{C_{a}}{\lambda_{\min}}\sum_{l=\tau_{1}}^{t}\prod_{j=l}^{t}\left(1-\frac{C_{a}}{j+C_{b}s\log(2d/s)}\right)\frac{1}{l+C_{b}s\log(2d/s)}\boldsymbol{\varXi}_{2}^{(l)}\\
&~~~~~+\frac{C_{a}^{2}}{\lambda_{\min}^{2}}\sum_{l=\tau_{1}}^{t}\prod_{j=l}^{t}\left(1-\frac{C_{a}}{j+C_{b}s\log(2d/s)}\right)\frac{1}{(l+C_{b}s\log(2d/s))^{2}}\boldsymbol{\varXi}_{3}^{(l)}.\end{split}

(22)

It is worth noting that at
\tau_{1}
, it has

\left\|\left[{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{\tau_{1}}-{\boldsymbol{\beta}}^{*}\right]_{{\mathcal{S}}_{1}}\right\|^{2}=\left\|\left[{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{\tau_{1}}-{\boldsymbol{\beta}}^{*}\right]_{{\mathcal{S}}_{0}}\right\|^{2}+\big\|[{\boldsymbol{\beta}}^{*}]_{{\mathcal{S}}_{1}\setminus{\mathcal{S}}_{0}}\big\|^{2}.

We then insert the above equation of
\left\|\left[{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{\tau_{1}}-{\boldsymbol{\beta}}^{*}\right]_{{\mathcal{S}}_{0}}\right\|^{2}
into the Equation
22
and accumulate the update until
t=0
, which yields

\|[{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{t+1}-{\boldsymbol{\beta}}^{*}]_{{\mathcal{S}}_{1}}\|^{2}\leq\underbrace{\prod_{j=0}^{t}\left(1-\frac{C_{a}}{j+C_{b}s\log(2d/s)}\right)\left\|\left[{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{0}-{\boldsymbol{\beta}}^{*}\right]_{{\mathcal{S}}_{0}}\right\|^{2}}_{\boldsymbol{\mathit{B}}_{1}}\\
+\underbrace{\prod_{j=\tau_{1}}^{t}\left(1-\frac{C_{a}}{j+C_{b}s\log(2d/s)}\right)\left\|\left[{\boldsymbol{\beta}}^{*}\right]_{{\mathcal{S}}_{1}\setminus{\mathcal{S}}_{0}}\right\|^{2}}_{\boldsymbol{\mathit{B}}_{2}}\\
+\underbrace{2s\sigma^{2}C_{a}^{2}\frac{\lambda_{\max}}{\lambda_{\min}^{2}}\sum_{l=0}^{t}\prod_{j=l}^{t}\left(1-\frac{C_{a}}{j+C_{b}s\log(2d/s)}\right)\left(\frac{1}{l+C_{b}s\log(2d/s)}\right)^{2}}_{\boldsymbol{\mathit{B}}_{3}}\\
+\underbrace{C_{a}\sum_{l=0}^{t}\prod_{j=l}^{t}\left(1-\frac{C_{a}}{j+C_{b}s\log(2d/s)}\right)\frac{1}{l+C_{b}s\log(2d/s)}\|[{\boldsymbol{\beta}}^{*}]_{{\mathcal{S}}^{*}\setminus{\mathcal{S}}_{l}}\|^{2}\cdot\frac{(\lambda_{\max,s}^{\textsf{Off}})^{2}}{\lambda_{\min}^{2}}}_{\boldsymbol{\mathit{B}}_{4}}\\
-\underbrace{2\frac{C_{a}}{\lambda_{\min}}\sum_{l=0}^{t}\prod_{j=l}^{t}\left(1-\frac{C_{a}}{j+C_{b}s\log(2d/s)}\right)\frac{1}{l+C_{b}s\log(2d/s)}\boldsymbol{\varXi}_{1}^{(l)}}_{\boldsymbol{\mathit{B}}_{5}}\\
+\underbrace{2\frac{C_{a}}{\lambda_{\min}}\sum_{l=0}^{t}\prod_{j=l}^{t}\left(1-\frac{C_{a}}{j+C_{b}s\log(2d/s)}\right)\frac{1}{l+C_{b}s\log(2d/s)}\boldsymbol{\varXi}_{2}^{(l)}}_{\boldsymbol{\mathit{B}}_{6}}\\
+\underbrace{\frac{C_{a}^{2}}{\lambda_{\min}^{2}}\sum_{l=0}^{t}\prod_{j=l}^{t}\left(1-\frac{C_{a}}{j+C_{b}s\log(2d/s)}\right)\frac{1}{(l+C_{b}s\log(2d/s))^{2}}\boldsymbol{\varXi}_{3}^{(l)}}_{\boldsymbol{\mathit{B}}_{7}}.

Firstly, we bound the term
\boldsymbol{\mathit{B}}_{1}
via Lemma
16
,

\displaystyle\boldsymbol{\mathit{B}}_{1}\leq\frac{C_{b}s\log(2d/s)}{t+1+C_{b}s\log(2d/s)}\|[{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{0}-{\boldsymbol{\beta}}^{*}]_{{\mathcal{S}}_{0}}\|^{2}\leq\frac{C_{b}s\log(2d/s)}{t+1+C_{b}s\log(2d/s)}\frac{\sigma^{2}}{\lambda_{\max}}.

Regarding term
\boldsymbol{\mathit{B}}_{2}
, we still apply Lemma
16
and it has

\displaystyle\boldsymbol{\mathit{B}}_{2}\leq\left(\frac{\tau_{1}+C_{b}s\log(2d/s)}{t+C_{b}s\log(2d/s)}\right)^{C_{a}}\|[{\boldsymbol{\beta}}^{*}]_{{\mathcal{S}}_{1}\setminus{\mathcal{S}}_{0}}\|^{2}.

As for term
{\mathbf{B}}_{3}
, it has

\prod_{j=l}^{t}\left(1-\frac{C_{a}}{j+C_{b}s\log(2d/s)}\right)\left(\frac{1}{l+C_{b}s\log(2d/s)}\right)^{2}\sigma^{2}\\
\leq\left(\frac{1}{t+1+C_{b}s\log(2d/s)}\right)^{C_{a}}\left(l+C_{b}s\log(2d/s)\right)^{C_{a}-2}\sigma^{2}.

Then, by Lemma
17
, it can be bounded with

\displaystyle\boldsymbol{\mathit{B}}_{3}\leq C_{a}\frac{\lambda_{\max}}{\lambda_{\min}^{2}}\frac{2s}{t+1+C_{b}s\log(2d/s)}\sigma^{2}.

In a similar fashion, we have the following bound for
\boldsymbol{\mathit{B}}_{4}
,

\displaystyle\boldsymbol{\mathit{B}}_{4}\leq\left(\frac{\lambda_{\max,s}^{\textsf{Off}}}{\lambda_{\min}}\right)^{2}\cdot\|[{\boldsymbol{\beta}}^{*}]_{{\mathcal{S}}^{*}\setminus{\mathcal{S}}_{1}}\|^{2}+\left(\frac{\tau_{1}+C_{b}s\log(2d/s)}{t+C_{b}s\log(2d/s)}\right)^{C_{a}}\left(\frac{\lambda_{\max,s}^{\textsf{Off}}}{\lambda_{\min}}\right)^{2}\cdot\|[{\boldsymbol{\beta}}^{*}]_{{\mathcal{S}}_{1}\setminus{\mathcal{S}}_{0}}\|^{2}.

We then consider the term
\boldsymbol{\mathit{B}}_{5}
. Specifically, the conditional Orlicz norm can be bounded with

\displaystyle\|\boldsymbol{\varXi}_{1}^{(l)}|{\mathcal{F}}_{l}\|_{\Psi_{1}}

\displaystyle\leq\lambda_{\max}\|[{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{l}-{\boldsymbol{\beta}}^{*}]_{{\mathcal{S}}_{l}}\|^{2}+\lambda_{\max,s}^{\textsf{Off}}\cdot\|[{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{l}-{\boldsymbol{\beta}}^{*}]_{{\mathcal{S}}_{l}}\|\cdot\|[{\boldsymbol{\beta}}^{*}]_{{\mathcal{S}}^{*}\setminus{\mathcal{S}}_{l}}\|

\displaystyle\leq 2\lambda_{\max}\|[{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{l}-{\boldsymbol{\beta}}^{*}]_{{\mathcal{S}}_{l}}\|^{2}+\frac{(\lambda_{\max,s}^{\textsf{Off}})^{2}}{\lambda_{\max}}\|[{\boldsymbol{\beta}}^{*}]_{{\mathcal{S}}^{*}\setminus{\mathcal{S}}_{l}}\|^{2}.

By Lemma
9
, under event
\cup_{l=0}^{t}\{{\mathcal{E}}_{l}\}
, we have

{\mathbb{P}}\left(\left|\sum_{l=0}^{t}\prod_{j=l}^{t}\left(1-\frac{C_{a}}{j+C_{b}s\log(2d/s)}\right)\frac{1}{l+C_{b}s\log(2d/s)}\boldsymbol{\varXi}_{1}^{(l)}\right|\geq c_{1}\frac{C^{*}}{C_{a}}\frac{(s\log(2d/s)+\delta_{t})\sigma^{2}}{t+1+C_{b}s\log(2d/s)}\right.\\
\left.+\frac{c_{1}}{C_{a}}\frac{(\lambda_{\max,s}^{\textsf{Off}})^{2}}{\lambda_{\min}}\|[{\boldsymbol{\beta}}^{*}]_{{\mathcal{S}}^{*}\setminus{\mathcal{S}}_{1}}\|^{2}+\lambda_{\min}\frac{c_{1}}{C_{a}}\left(\frac{\tau_{1}+C_{b}s\log(2d/s)}{t+1+C_{b}s\log(2d/s)}\right)^{C_{a}-2}\|[{\boldsymbol{\beta}}^{*}]_{{\mathcal{S}}_{1}\setminus{\mathcal{S}}_{0}}\|^{2}\right)\\
\leq\exp\left(-c_{2}\frac{C_{b}s\log(2d/s)+t}{C_{a}}\frac{\lambda_{\min}^{2}}{\lambda_{\max}^{2}}\right),

under which we have

|\boldsymbol{\mathit{B}}_{5}|\leq c_{1}\frac{C^{*}}{\lambda_{\min}}\frac{(s\log(2d/s)+\delta_{t})\sigma^{2}}{t+1+C_{b}s\log(2d/s)}+c_{1}\left(\frac{\lambda_{\max,s}^{\textsf{Off}}}{\lambda_{\min}}\right)^{2}\|[{\boldsymbol{\beta}}^{*}]_{{\mathcal{S}}^{*}\setminus{\mathcal{S}}_{1}}\|^{2}\\
+c_{1}\left(\frac{\tau_{1}+C_{b}s\log(2d/s)}{t+1+C_{b}s\log(2d/s)}\right)^{C_{a}-2}\cdot\|[{\boldsymbol{\beta}}^{*}]_{{\mathcal{S}}_{1}\setminus{\mathcal{S}}_{0}}\|^{2}.

As for the term
\boldsymbol{\mathit{B}}_{6}
, we have

\displaystyle\|\boldsymbol{\varXi}_{2}^{(t)}|{\mathcal{F}}_{t}\|_{\Psi_{1}}\leq\sqrt{\lambda_{\max}}\sigma\|[{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{t}-{\boldsymbol{\beta}}^{*}]_{{\mathcal{S}}_{t}}\|,

which leads to

\left\|\prod_{j=l}^{t}\left(1-\frac{C_{a}}{j+C_{b}s\log(2d/s)}\right)\frac{1}{l+C_{b}s\log(2d/s)}\boldsymbol{\varXi}_{2}^{(l)}\big|{\mathcal{F}}_{l}\right\|_{\Psi_{1}}\\
\leq C\sqrt{\lambda_{\max}}\sigma\frac{(l+C_{b}s\log(2d/s))^{C_{a}-1}}{(t+1+C_{b}s\log(2d/s))^{C_{a}}}\cdot\|[{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{l}-{\boldsymbol{\beta}}^{*}]_{{\mathcal{S}}_{l}}\|.

Then under event
\cup_{l=0}^{t}\{{\mathcal{E}}_{l}\}
, by Lemma
9
, we have

{\mathbb{P}}\left(\left|\sum_{l=0}^{t}\prod_{j=l}^{t}\left(1-\frac{C_{a}}{j+C_{b}s\log(2d/s)}\right)\frac{1}{l+C_{b}s\log(2d/s)}\boldsymbol{\varXi}_{2}^{(l)}\right|\geq c_{1}\frac{C^{*}}{C_{a}}\frac{(s\log(2d/s)+\delta_{t+1})\sigma^{2}}{t+1+C_{b}s\log(2d/s)}\right.\\
\left.+\frac{c_{1}}{C_{a}}\frac{(\lambda_{\max,s}^{\textsf{Off}})^{2}}{\lambda_{\min}}\|[{\boldsymbol{\beta}}^{*}]_{{\mathcal{S}}^{*}\setminus{\mathcal{S}}_{1}}\|^{2}+\lambda_{\min}\frac{c_{1}}{C_{a}}\left(\frac{\tau_{1}+C_{b}s\log(2d/s)}{t+1+C_{b}s\log(2d/s)}\right)^{C_{a}-2}\|[{\boldsymbol{\beta}}^{*}]_{{\mathcal{S}}_{1}\setminus{\mathcal{S}}_{0}}\|^{2}\right)\\
\leq\exp\left(-c_{2}\min\left\{(s\log(2d/s)+\delta_{t+1})\frac{C^{*}}{C_{a}}\frac{\lambda_{\min}}{\lambda_{\max}},\sqrt{s\log(2d/s)+\delta_{t+1}}\sqrt{C_{b}s\log(2d/s)+t}\frac{\sqrt{C^{*}}}{C_{a}}\frac{\sqrt{\lambda_{\min}}}{\sqrt{\lambda_{\max}}}\right\}\right),

which implies the following bound

|\boldsymbol{\mathit{B}}_{6}|\leq c_{1}\frac{C^{*}}{\lambda_{\min}}\frac{(s\log(2d/s)+\delta_{t+1})\sigma^{2}}{t+1+C_{b}s\log(2d/s)}\\
+c_{1}\frac{(\lambda_{\max,s}^{\textsf{Off}})^{2}}{\lambda_{\min}^{2}}\|[{\boldsymbol{\beta}}^{*}]_{{\mathcal{S}}^{*}\setminus{\mathcal{S}}_{1}}\|^{2}+c_{1}\left(\frac{\tau_{1}+C_{b}s\log(2d/s)}{t+1+C_{b}s\log(2d/s)}\right)^{C_{a}-2}\cdot\|[{\boldsymbol{\beta}}^{*}]_{{\mathcal{S}}_{1}\setminus{\mathcal{S}}_{0}}\|^{2}.

Recall the definition
{\mathcal{E}}_{t}^{X}:=\{\max_{|{\mathcal{S}}|\leq s}\|[{\mathbf{X}}_{t}]_{{\mathcal{S}}}\|^{2}\leq C\lambda_{\max}(s\log(2d/s)+\delta_{t})\}
(defined in Section
B.1
). Hence, under
{\mathcal{E}}_{t}^{X}
, we have

\displaystyle\|\boldsymbol{\varXi}_{3}^{(t)}|{\mathcal{F}}_{t},{\mathcal{E}}_{t}^{X}\|_{\Psi_{1}}\leq C(s\log(2d/s)+\delta_{t})\lambda_{\max}\sigma^{2}.

With a similar analysis to Section
B.1
, we have with probability over
1-\exp(-c_{2}(s\log(2d/s)+t/C_{b}))
,

|\boldsymbol{\mathit{B}}_{7}|\leq c_{1}C^{*}\frac{1}{\lambda_{\min}}\frac{s\log(2d/s)+\delta_{t+1}}{t+1+C_{b}s\log(2d/s)}\sigma^{2}.

Thus, altogether, we finished proving the convergence dynamics for
t\in[\tau_{1},\tau_{2}-1]
,

\|[{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{t+1}-{\boldsymbol{\beta}}^{*}]_{{\mathcal{S}}_{1}}\|^{2}\leq C^{*}\frac{s\log(2d/s)}{t+1+C_{b}s\log(2d/s)}\sigma^{2}+C\left(\frac{\lambda_{\max,s}^{\textsf{Off}}}{\lambda_{\min}}\right)^{2}\|[{\boldsymbol{\beta}}^{*}]_{{\mathcal{S}}^{*}\setminus{\mathcal{S}}_{1}}\|^{2}\\
+C\left(\frac{\tau_{1}+C_{b}s\log(2d/s)}{t+1+C_{b}s\log(2d/s)}\right)^{C_{a}-2}\|[{\boldsymbol{\beta}}^{*}]_{{\mathcal{S}}_{1}\setminus{\mathcal{S}}_{0}}\|^{2}.

Appendix C
Proofs in Section
4

C.1
Proof of Lemma
4

Proof.

Recall that
\max_{i\in[K]}\|{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}-{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{*}\|\leq h_{0}
. Then by triangle inequality, we have

\displaystyle\left|\frac{{\mathbf{X}}^{\top}{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{*}}{\|{\mathbf{X}}\|}-\frac{{\mathbf{X}}^{\top}{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}}{\|{\mathbf{X}}\|}\right|\leq\|{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}-{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{*}\|\leq h_{0},\quad\left|\max_{j\neq i}\frac{{\mathbf{X}}^{\top}{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{j}^{*}}{\|{\mathbf{X}}\|}-\max_{j\neq i}\frac{{\mathbf{X}}^{\top}{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{j}}{\|{\mathbf{X}}\|}\right|\leq\max_{j\neq i}\|{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{j}-{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{j}^{*}\|\leq h_{0}.

Hence, for
{\mathbf{X}}\in{\mathcal{X}}\left(i,\left\{{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{j}\right\}\right)
, it has

\displaystyle\frac{{\mathbf{X}}^{\top}{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{*}}{\|{\mathbf{X}}\|}

\displaystyle=\frac{{\mathbf{X}}^{\top}{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}}{\|{\mathbf{X}}\|}+\left(\frac{{\mathbf{X}}^{\top}{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{*}}{\|{\mathbf{X}}\|}-\frac{{\mathbf{X}}^{\top}{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}}{\|{\mathbf{X}}\|}\right)\geq\max_{j\neq i}\frac{{\mathbf{X}}^{\top}{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{j}}{\|{\mathbf{X}}\|}-h_{0}\geq\max_{j\neq i}\frac{{\mathbf{X}}^{\top}{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{j}^{*}}{\|{\mathbf{X}}\|}-2h_{0},

which implies
{\mathbf{X}}\in{\mathcal{U}}_{i}(-2h_{0})
. Hence, we finish proving
{\mathcal{X}}\left(i,\left\{{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{j}\right\}\right)\subseteq{\mathcal{U}}_{i}(-2h_{0})
. On the other hand, if
{\mathbf{X}}\in{\mathcal{U}}_{i}(2h_{0})
, then we have

\displaystyle\frac{{\mathbf{X}}^{\top}{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}}{\|{\mathbf{X}}\|}=\frac{{\mathbf{X}}^{\top}{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{*}}{\|{\mathbf{X}}\|}+\left(\frac{{\mathbf{X}}^{\top}{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}}{\|{\mathbf{X}}\|}-\frac{{\mathbf{X}}^{\top}{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{*}}{\|{\mathbf{X}}\|}\right)\geq\max_{j\neq i}\frac{{\mathbf{X}}^{\top}{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{j}^{*}}{\|{\mathbf{X}}\|}+2h_{0}-h_{0}\geq\max_{j\neq i}\frac{{\mathbf{X}}^{\top}{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{j}}{\|{\mathbf{X}}\|},

which shows
{\mathbf{X}}\in{\mathcal{X}}\left(i,\left\{{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{j}\right\}\right)
. Thus, we complete the proof.
∎

C.2
Proof of Lemma
5

Proof of Lemma
5
.

We first prove claim
(1)
of Lemma
5
. For
j\in{\mathcal{A}}^{c}
, we have

\displaystyle\frac{{\mathbf{X}}_{t}^{\top}{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{j}}{\|{\mathbf{X}}_{t}\|}=\frac{{\mathbf{X}}_{t}^{\top}{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{j}^{*}}{\|{\mathbf{X}}_{t}\|}+\frac{{\mathbf{X}}_{t}^{\top}\left({\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{j}-{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{j}^{*}\right)}{\|{\mathbf{X}}_{t}\|}=\frac{\max_{i\in[K]}{\mathbf{X}}_{t}^{\top}{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{*}}{\|{\mathbf{X}}_{t}\|}-\frac{\max_{i\in[K]}{\mathbf{X}}_{t}^{\top}{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{*}-{\mathbf{X}}_{t}^{\top}{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{j}^{*}}{\|{\mathbf{X}}_{t}\|}+\frac{{\mathbf{X}}_{t}^{\top}\left({\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{j}-{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{j}^{*}\right)}{\|{\mathbf{X}}_{t}\|}.

Assumption
5
guarantees
\frac{\max_{i\in[K]}{\mathbf{X}}_{t}^{\top}{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{*}-{\mathbf{X}}_{t}^{\top}{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{j}^{*}}{\|{\mathbf{X}}_{t}\|}\geq h
, a.s.. Thus, together with
{\mathbf{X}}_{t}^{\top}\left({\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{j}-{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{j}^{*}\right)\leq\|{\mathbf{X}}_{t}\|\cdot\|{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}-{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{*}\|
and under the event
\{\|{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}-{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{*}\|\leq\frac{h}{2},\ i\in[K]\}
, it arrives at

\displaystyle\frac{{\mathbf{X}}_{t}^{\top}{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{j}}{\|{\mathbf{X}}_{t}\|}\leq\frac{\max_{i\in[K]}{\mathbf{X}}_{t}^{\top}{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{*}}{\|{\mathbf{X}}_{t}\|}-\frac{h}{2}<\frac{\max_{i\in[K]}{\mathbf{X}}_{t}^{\top}{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{*}}{\|{\mathbf{X}}_{t}\|},

which implies
{\mathcal{X}}(j,\{{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}\})=\emptyset
for
j\in{\mathcal{A}}^{c}
.

Denote
h_{{\mathcal{A}}}:=\max_{i\in{\mathcal{A}}}\|{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}-{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{*}\|
. Further, under the event
\{\|{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}-{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{*}\|\leq\frac{h}{2},\ i\in[K]\}
, for any
i\in{\mathcal{A}}
, if
{\mathbf{X}}\in{\mathcal{X}}\left(i,\left\{{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{j}\right\}\right)
, it has

\displaystyle\frac{{\mathbf{X}}^{\top}{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{*}}{\|{\mathbf{X}}\|}

\displaystyle=\frac{{\mathbf{X}}^{\top}{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}}{\|{\mathbf{X}}\|}+\left(\frac{{\mathbf{X}}^{\top}{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{*}}{\|{\mathbf{X}}\|}-\frac{{\mathbf{X}}^{\top}{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}}{\|{\mathbf{X}}\|}\right)\geq\max_{j\neq i,j\in{\mathcal{A}}}\frac{{\mathbf{X}}^{\top}{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{j}}{\|{\mathbf{X}}\|}-h_{{\mathcal{A}}}\geq\max_{j\neq i,j\in{\mathcal{A}}}\frac{{\mathbf{X}}^{\top}{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{j}^{*}}{\|{\mathbf{X}}\|}-2h_{{\mathcal{A}}},

and by Assumption
5
, it has

\max_{j\in{\mathcal{A}}}\frac{{\mathbf{X}}^{\top}{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{j}^{*}}{\|{\mathbf{X}}\|}\geq\max_{j\in{\mathcal{A}}^{c}}\frac{{\mathbf{X}}^{\top}{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{j}^{*}}{\|{\mathbf{X}}\|}+h.

The above two equation together imply
{\mathbf{X}}\in{\mathcal{U}}_{i}(-2h_{{\mathcal{A}}})
. Hence, we finish proving
{\mathcal{X}}\left(i,\left\{{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{j}\right\}\right)\subseteq{\mathcal{U}}_{i}(-2h_{{\mathcal{A}}})
. On the other hand, for any
{\mathbf{X}}\in{\mathcal{U}}_{i}(2h_{{\mathcal{A}}})
, we have

\displaystyle\frac{{\mathbf{X}}^{\top}{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}}{\|{\mathbf{X}}\|}=\frac{{\mathbf{X}}^{\top}{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{*}}{\|{\mathbf{X}}\|}+\left(\frac{{\mathbf{X}}^{\top}{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}}{\|{\mathbf{X}}\|}-\frac{{\mathbf{X}}^{\top}{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{*}}{\|{\mathbf{X}}\|}\right)\geq\max_{j\neq i,j\in{\mathcal{A}}}\frac{{\mathbf{X}}^{\top}{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{j}^{*}}{\|{\mathbf{X}}\|}+2h_{{\mathcal{A}}}-h_{{\mathcal{A}}}.

Then, by triangle inequality
\max_{j\neq i,j\in{\mathcal{A}}}\frac{{\mathbf{X}}^{\top}{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{j}^{*}}{\|{\mathbf{X}}\|}\geq\max_{j\neq i,j\in{\mathcal{A}}}\frac{{\mathbf{X}}^{\top}{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{j}}{\|{\mathbf{X}}\|}-h_{{\mathcal{A}}}
, we have
\frac{{\mathbf{X}}^{\top}{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}}{\|{\mathbf{X}}\|}\geq\max_{j\neq i,j\in{\mathcal{A}}}\frac{{\mathbf{X}}^{\top}{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{j}}{\|{\mathbf{X}}\|}
. Moreover, under the event
\{\|{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}-{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{*}\|\leq\frac{h}{2},\ i\in[K]\}
, we have
{\mathbf{X}}\notin{\mathcal{X}}\left(k,\left\{{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{j}\right\}\right)
for all
k\in{\mathcal{A}}^{c}
. Hence, we finish proving
{\mathbf{X}}\in{\mathcal{X}}\left(i,\left\{{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{j}\right\}\right)
.

We then only need to prove claim
(2)
of Lemma
5
. By triangle inequality, for all
{\mathbf{X}}\in{\mathbb{R}}^{d}
, it has

\displaystyle{\mathbf{X}}^{\top}{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}

\displaystyle\geq{\mathbf{X}}^{\top}{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{*}-\left|{\mathbf{X}}^{\top}{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}-{\mathbf{X}}^{\top}{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{*}\right|

\displaystyle\geq\max_{j\neq i}{\mathbf{X}}^{\top}{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{j}-2\max_{j}\left|{\mathbf{X}}^{\top}{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{j}-{\mathbf{X}}^{\top}{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{j}^{*}\right|+\left({\mathbf{X}}^{\top}{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{*}-\max_{j\neq i}{\mathbf{X}}^{\top}{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{j}^{*}\right).

It further implies that

{\mathbb{I}}\left\{{\mathbf{X}}^{\top}{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}\geq\max_{j\neq i}{\mathbf{X}}^{\top}{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{j}\right\}\\
\geq{\mathbb{I}}\left\{2\max_{j}\left|{\mathbf{X}}^{\top}{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{j}-{\mathbf{X}}^{\top}{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{j}^{*}\right|\cdot\|{\mathbf{X}}\|^{-1}\leq\left({\mathbf{X}}^{\top}{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{*}-\max_{j\neq i}{\mathbf{X}}^{\top}{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{j}^{*}\right)\cdot\|{\mathbf{X}}\|^{-1}\right\}.

Thus, using the above equation, we have the following bound

\displaystyle~~~~{\mathbb{E}}\left\{{\mathbb{I}}{\{{\mathbf{X}}_{t}^{\top}{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}\geq\max_{j}{\mathbf{X}}_{t}^{\top}{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{j}\}}\cdot{\mathbf{X}}_{t}{\mathbf{X}}_{t}^{\top}\bigg|{\mathcal{F}}_{t}\right\}

\displaystyle\succeq{\mathbb{E}}\left\{{\mathbb{I}}{\left\{2\max_{j}\left|{\mathbf{X}}_{t}^{\top}{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{j}-{\mathbf{X}}_{t}^{\top}{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{j}^{*}\right|\cdot\|{\mathbf{X}}_{t}\|^{-1}\leq\left({\mathbf{X}}_{t}^{\top}{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{*}-\max_{j\neq i}{\mathbf{X}}_{t}^{\top}{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{j}^{*}\right)\cdot\|{\mathbf{X}}_{t}\|^{-1}\right\}}\cdot{\mathbf{X}}_{t}{\mathbf{X}}_{t}^{\top}\bigg|{\mathcal{F}}_{t}\right\}.

Recall that under Assumption
5
,
{\mathbf{X}}^{\top}{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{*}-\max_{j\neq i}{\mathbf{X}}^{\top}{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{j}^{*}\geq h\|{\mathbf{X}}\|
holds for all
{\mathbf{X}}\in{\mathcal{U}}_{i}(h)
. Thus, we restrict the right hand side to region
{\mathcal{U}}_{i}(h)
and then it arrives at

{\mathbb{E}}\left\{{\mathbb{I}}{\{{\mathbf{X}}_{t}^{\top}{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}\geq\max_{j}{\mathbf{X}}_{t}^{\top}{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{j}\}}\cdot{\mathbf{X}}_{t}{\mathbf{X}}_{t}^{\top}\bigg|{\mathcal{F}}_{t}\right\}\\
\succeq{\mathbb{E}}\left\{{\mathbb{I}}{\left\{2\max_{j}\left|{\mathbf{X}}_{t}^{\top}{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{j}-{\mathbf{X}}_{t}^{\top}{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{j}^{*}\right|\cdot\|{\mathbf{X}}_{t}\|^{-1}\leq h\right\}}\cdot{\mathbf{X}}_{t}{\mathbf{X}}_{t}^{\top}\cdot{\mathbb{I}}\left\{{\mathbf{X}}_{t}\in{\mathcal{U}}_{i}(h)\right\}\bigg|{\mathcal{F}}_{t}\right\}.

On the other hand, it’s obvious that
\max_{j}|{\mathbf{X}}_{t}^{\top}{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{j}-{\mathbf{X}}_{t}^{\top}{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{j}^{*}|\cdot\|{\mathbf{X}}_{t}\|^{-1}\leq\max_{j}\|{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{j}-{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{j}^{*}\|
. Then we have

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}\left\{{\mathbb{I}}{\{{\mathbf{X}}_{t}^{\top}{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}\geq\max_{j}{\mathbf{X}}_{t}^{\top}{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{j}\}}\cdot{\mathbf{X}}_{t}{\mathbf{X}}_{t}^{\top}\bigg|{\mathcal{F}}_{t}\right\}

\displaystyle\succeq{\mathbb{I}}\left\{\max_{j}\|{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{j}-{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{j}^{*}\|\leq\frac{h}{2}\right\}\cdot{\mathbb{E}}\left\{{\mathbf{X}}_{t}{\mathbf{X}}_{t}^{\top}\cdot{\mathbb{I}}\left\{{\mathbf{X}}_{t}\in{\mathcal{U}}_{i}\right\}\big|{\mathcal{F}}_{t}\right\},

which proves the first statement of Lemma
5
. It remains to discuss
{\mathcal{A}}^{c}
. Thus, we complete the proof.
∎

C.3
Proof of Theorem
4

Recall
{\mathcal{F}}_{t}=\sigma(Y_{t-1},{\mathbf{X}}_{t-1},\alpha_{t-1},\ldots)
. The following proposition justifies the conditional expectation of the estimation error.

Proposition 3
.

Under the same conditions as Theorem
4
, for
i\in{\mathcal{A}}
, it has

{\mathbb{E}}\left\{\|{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{(t+1)}-{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{*}\|^{2}\big|{\mathcal{F}}_{t}\right\}\leq\|{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{(t)}-{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{*}\|^{2}-2\eta_{t}\frac{\pi_{t}}{K}\lambda_{\min}\|{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{(t)}-{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{*}\|^{2}+2\eta_{t}^{2}d\lambda_{\max}^{2}\|{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{(t)}-{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{*}\|^{2}\\
-2\eta_{t}(1-\pi_{t})\lambda_{\min}\|{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{(t)}-{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{*}\|^{2}\cdot{\mathbb{I}}\left\{\max_{j}\|{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{j}^{(t)}-{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{*}\|\leq\frac{h}{2}\right\}+2\eta_{t}^{2}d\lambda_{\max}\sigma^{2},

and for
i\in{\mathcal{A}}^{c}
, we have

{\mathbb{E}}\left\{\|{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{(t+1)}-{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{*}\|^{2}\big|{\mathcal{F}}_{t}\right\}\leq\|{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{(t)}-{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{*}\|^{2}-2\eta_{t}\frac{\pi_{t}}{K}\lambda_{\min}\|{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{(t)}-{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{*}\|^{2}\\
+2\eta_{t}^{2}d\lambda_{\max}\sigma^{2}\cdot\left(\frac{\pi_{t}}{K}+(1-\pi_{t})\cdot{\mathbb{I}}\left\{\max_{i\in[K]}\|{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{(t)}-{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{*}\|\geq\frac{h}{2}\right\}\right)\\
+2\eta_{t}^{2}d\lambda_{\max}^{2}\|{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{(t)}-{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{*}\|^{2}\cdot\left(\frac{\pi_{t}}{K}+(1-\pi_{t})\cdot{\mathbb{I}}\left\{\max_{i\in[K]}\|{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{(t)}-{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{*}\|\geq\frac{h}{2}\right\}\right),

where
C>0
is some constant.

Proof of Proposition
3
.

Indeed, the update can be characterized as follows,

\displaystyle{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{(t+1)}-{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{*}={\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{(t)}-{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{*}-\eta_{t}\ {\mathbb{I}}{\{a_{t}=i\}}\cdot({\mathbf{X}}_{t}^{\top}{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{(t)}-Y_{t})\ {\mathbf{X}}_{t}.

Then the
L_{2}
normed estimation error rate has,

\|{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{(t+1)}-{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{*}\|^{2}=\|{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{(t)}-{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{*}\|^{2}-2\eta_{t}\ {\mathbb{I}}{\{a_{t}=i\}}\cdot({\mathbf{X}}_{t}^{\top}{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{(t)}-{\mathbf{X}}_{t}^{\top}{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{*})^{2}\\
-2\eta_{t}\ {\mathbb{I}}{\{a_{t}=i\}}\ \xi_{t}\ ({\mathbf{X}}_{t}^{\top}{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{(t)}-{\mathbf{X}}_{t}^{\top}{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{*})+\eta_{t}^{2}\ {\mathbb{I}}{\{a_{t}=i\}}\cdot({\mathbf{X}}_{t}^{\top}{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{(t)}-Y_{t})^{2}\ \|{\mathbf{X}}_{t}\|^{2}.

Recall
{\mathcal{F}}_{t}^{+}:=\sigma\left({\mathbf{X}}_{t},Y_{t-1},{\mathbf{X}}_{t-1},\alpha_{t-1},\dots\right)
and we first consider the conditional expectation of the above equation. First of all, the indicator function has

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}\left\{{\mathbb{I}}{\{a_{t}=i\}}\big|{\mathcal{F}}_{t}^{+}\right\}

\displaystyle={\mathbb{E}}\left\{{\mathbb{I}}{\{a_{t}=i\}}\cdot{\mathbb{I}}\left\{\alpha_{t}=1\right\}\big|{\mathcal{F}}_{t}^{+}\right\}+{\mathbb{E}}\left\{{\mathbb{I}}{\{a_{t}=i\}}\cdot{\mathbb{I}}\left\{\alpha_{t}=0\right\}\big|{\mathcal{F}}_{t}^{+}\right\}

\displaystyle=\frac{\pi_{t}}{K}+(1-\pi_{t})\cdot{\mathbb{I}}\left\{i\in\arg\max_{j}{\mathbf{X}}_{t}^{\top}{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{j}^{(t)}\right\}.

Secondly, the conditional expectation of
({\mathbf{X}}_{t}^{\top}{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{(t)}-Y_{t})^{2}
is given by

{\mathbb{E}}\left\{\left({\mathbf{X}}_{t}^{\top}{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{(t)}-Y_{t}\right)^{2}\bigg|{\mathcal{F}}_{t}^{+}\right\}\leq\left({\mathbf{X}}_{t}^{\top}{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{(t)}-{\mathbf{X}}_{t}^{\top}{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{*}\right)^{2}+\sigma^{2}.

Thus, altogether, we have

{\mathbb{E}}\left\{\left\|{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{(t+1)}-{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{*}\right\|^{2}\bigg|{\mathcal{F}}_{t}^{+}\right\}\\
=\left\|{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{(t)}-{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{*}\right\|^{2}-2\eta_{t}\ \left(\frac{\pi_{t}}{K}+(1-\pi_{t})\cdot{\mathbb{I}}\left\{i\in\arg\max_{j}{\mathbf{X}}_{t}^{\top}{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{j}^{(t)}\right\}\right)\cdot\left({\mathbf{X}}_{t}^{\top}{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{(t)}-{\mathbf{X}}_{t}^{\top}{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{*}\right)^{2}\\
+\eta_{t}^{2}\ \left(\frac{\pi_{t}}{K}+(1-\pi_{t})\cdot{\mathbb{I}}\left\{i=\arg\max_{j}{\mathbf{X}}_{t}^{\top}{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{j}^{(t)}\right\}\right)\cdot\left(({\mathbf{X}}_{t}^{\top}{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{(t)}-{\mathbf{X}}_{t}^{\top}{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{*})^{2}+\sigma^{2}\right)\ \left\|{\mathbf{X}}_{t}\right\|^{2}.

Then, the expectation conditional on
{\mathcal{F}}_{t}
has,

{\mathbb{E}}\left\{\left\|{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{(t+1)}-{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{*}\right\|^{2}\bigg|{\mathcal{F}}_{t}\right\}\leq\|{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{(t)}-{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{*}\|^{2}-2\eta_{t}\frac{\pi_{t}}{K}\lambda_{\min}\|{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{(t)}-{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{*}\|^{2}\\
-2\eta_{t}(1-\pi_{t})\cdot\underbrace{{\mathbb{E}}\left\{{\mathbb{I}}{\{i=\arg\max_{j}{\mathbf{X}}_{t}^{\top}{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{j}^{(t)}\}}\cdot({\mathbf{X}}_{t}^{\top}{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{(t)}-{\mathbf{X}}_{t}^{\top}{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{*})^{2}\big|{\mathcal{F}}_{t}\right\}}_{{\mathbf{A}}_{1}}\\
+\eta_{t}^{2}\cdot\frac{\pi_{t}}{K}\ \sigma^{2}\cdot{\mathbb{E}}\left\|{\mathbf{X}}_{t}\right\|^{2}+\eta_{t}^{2}\cdot\frac{\pi_{t}}{K}\cdot\underbrace{{\mathbb{E}}\left\{({\mathbf{X}}_{t}^{\top}{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{(t)}-{\mathbf{X}}_{t}^{\top}{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{*})^{2}\cdot\left\|{\mathbf{X}}_{t}\right\|^{2}\big|{\mathcal{F}}_{t}\right\}}_{{\mathbf{A}}_{2}}\\
+\eta_{t}^{2}\cdot(1-\pi_{t})\cdot\underbrace{{\mathbb{E}}\left\{{\mathbb{I}}{\{i=\arg\max_{j}{\mathbf{X}}_{t}^{\top}{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{j}^{(t)}\}}\cdot({\mathbf{X}}_{t}^{\top}{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{(t)}-{\mathbf{X}}_{t}^{\top}{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{*})^{2}\cdot\|{\mathbf{X}}_{t}\|^{2}\big|{\mathcal{F}}_{t}\right\}}_{{\mathbf{A}}_{3}}\\
+\eta_{t}^{2}\cdot(1-\pi_{t})\cdot\sigma^{2}\cdot\underbrace{{\mathbb{E}}\left\{{\mathbb{I}}{\{i=\arg\max_{j}{\mathbf{X}}_{t}^{\top}{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{j}^{(t)}\}}\cdot\|{\mathbf{X}}_{t}\|^{2}\big|{\mathcal{F}}_{t}\right\}}_{{\mathbf{A}}_{4}}.

We shall discuss the bound of
{\mathbf{A}}_{1},{\mathbf{A}}_{2},{\mathbf{A}}_{3},{\mathbf{A}}_{4}
separately depending on whether
i\in{\mathcal{A}}
or
i\in{\mathcal{A}}^{c}
.

1.

For
i\in{\mathcal{A}}
.

Bound of
{\mathbf{A}}_{1}

Lemma
5
gives

{\mathbf{A}}_{1}\geq\lambda_{\min}\|{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{(t)}-{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}\|^{2}\cdot{\mathbb{I}}\left\{\max_{i}\|{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{(t)}-{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{*}\|\leq\frac{h}{2}\right\}.

Bound of
{\mathbf{A}}_{2}
To bound
{\mathbf{A}}_{2}
, we know that
{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{t}\in{\mathcal{F}}_{t}
. Then under Assumption
, we have

\displaystyle{\mathbf{A}}_{2}\leq\|{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{(t)}-{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{*}\|^{2}\cdot\left\|{\mathbb{E}}\left\{{\mathbf{X}}_{t}{\mathbf{X}}_{t}^{\top}\cdot\left\|{\mathbf{X}}_{t}\right\|^{2}\right\}\big|{\mathcal{F}}_{t}\right\|\leq 2d\lambda_{\max}^{2}\|{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{(t)}-{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{*}\|^{2},

where
C
is some constant.

Bound of
{\mathbf{A}}_{3}
Similar to bound of
{\mathbf{A}}_{2}
, we have

\displaystyle{\mathbf{A}}_{3}\leq 2d\lambda_{\max}^{2}\|{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{(t)}-{\boldsymbol{\beta}}^{*}\|^{2}.

Bound of
{\mathbf{A}}_{4}
In a similar fashion, we have

\displaystyle{\mathbf{A}}_{4}\leq 2d\lambda_{\max}.

Thus, in all, the conditional expected estimation error has,

{\mathbb{E}}\left\{\|{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{(t+1)}-{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{*}\|^{2}\big|{\mathcal{F}}_{t}\right\}\leq\|{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{(t)}-{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{*}\|^{2}-2\eta_{t}\frac{\pi_{t}}{K}\lambda_{\min}\|{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{(t)}-{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{*}\|^{2}+2\eta_{t}^{2}d\lambda_{\max}\sigma^{2}\\
-2\eta_{t}(1-\pi_{t})\lambda_{\min}\|{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{(t)}-{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{*}\|^{2}\cdot{\mathbb{I}}\left\{\max_{i}\|{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{(t)}-{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{*}\|\leq\frac{h}{2}\right\}+2\eta_{t}^{2}d\lambda_{\max}^{2}\|{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{(t)}-{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{*}\|^{2}.

2.

For
i\in{\mathcal{A}}^{c}
.

Bound of
{\mathbf{A}}_{1}
We use
{\mathbf{A}}_{1}\geq 0
.

Bound of
{\mathbf{A}}_{2}
Its bound is same as the case when
i\in{\mathcal{A}}
.

Bound of
{\mathbf{A}}_{3}
Lemma
5
shows that when
\max_{i\in[K]}\|{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{(t)}-{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{*}\|\leq h/2
,

\arg\max_{i\in[K]}{\mathbf{X}}^{\top}{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{(t)}\subseteq{\mathcal{A}}.

Thus, we have
{\mathbf{A}}_{3}\leq Cd\lambda_{\max}^{2}\|{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{(t)}-{\boldsymbol{\beta}}^{*}\|^{2}\cdot{\mathbb{I}}\left\{\max_{i\in[K]}\|{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{(t)}-{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{*}\|\geq h/2\right\}
.

Bound of
{\mathbf{A}}_{4}
In a similar fashion, we have

\displaystyle{\mathbf{A}}_{4}\leq 2d\lambda_{\max}\cdot{\mathbb{I}}\left\{\max_{i\in[K]}\|{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{(t)}-{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{*}\|\geq h/2\right\}.

Thus, in all, the estimation error rate has

{\mathbb{E}}\left\{\|{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{(t+1)}-{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{*}\|^{2}\big|{\mathcal{F}}_{t}\right\}\leq\|{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{(t)}-{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{*}\|^{2}-2\eta_{t}\frac{\pi_{t}}{K}\lambda_{\min}\|{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{(t)}-{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{*}\|^{2}\\
+\eta_{t}^{2}d\lambda_{\max}\sigma^{2}\cdot\left(\frac{\pi_{t}}{K}+(1-\pi_{t})\cdot{\mathbb{I}}\left\{\max_{i\in[K]}\|{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{(t)}-{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{*}\|\geq\frac{h}{2}\right\}\right)\\
+2\eta_{t}^{2}d\lambda_{\max}^{2}\|{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{(t)}-{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{*}\|^{2}\cdot\left(\frac{\pi_{t}}{K}+(1-\pi_{t})\cdot{\mathbb{I}}\left\{\max_{i\in[K]}\|{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{(t)}-{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{*}\|\geq\frac{h}{2}\right\}\right),

which completes the proof.

∎

Then we are ready to prove Theorem
4
.

Proof of Theorem
4
.

Before discussing the convergence, we introduce

\displaystyle\|{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{(t+1)}-{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{*}\|^{2}-{\mathbb{E}}\left\{\|{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{(t+1)}-{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{*}\|^{2}\big|{\mathcal{F}}_{t}\right\}

\displaystyle~~~~~=-2\eta_{t}\underbrace{\left\{{\mathbb{I}}{\{a_{t}=i\}}\cdot({\mathbf{X}}_{t}^{\top}{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{(t)}-{\mathbf{X}}_{t}^{\top}{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{*})^{2}-{\mathbb{E}}\left\{{\mathbb{I}}{\{a_{t}=i\}}\cdot({\mathbf{X}}_{t}^{\top}{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{(t)}-{\mathbf{X}}_{t}^{\top}{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{*})^{2}\big|{\mathcal{F}}_{t}\right\}\right\}}_{=:\boldsymbol{\Xi}_{1}^{(t)}}

\displaystyle~~~~~~~-2\eta_{t}\underbrace{\left\{{\mathbb{I}}{\{a_{t}=i\}}\ \xi_{t}\ ({\mathbf{X}}_{t}^{\top}{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{(t)}-{\mathbf{X}}_{t}^{\top}{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{*})-{\mathbb{E}}\left\{{\mathbb{I}}{\{a_{t}=i\}}\ \xi_{t}\ ({\mathbf{X}}_{t}^{\top}{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{(t)}-{\mathbf{X}}_{t}^{\top}{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{*})\big|{\mathcal{F}}_{t}\right\}\right\}}_{=:\boldsymbol{\Xi}_{2}^{(t)}}

\displaystyle~~~~~~~+\eta_{t}^{2}\underbrace{\left\{{\mathbb{I}}{\{a_{t}=i\}}\cdot({\mathbf{X}}_{t}^{\top}{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{(t)}-Y_{t})^{2}\ \|{\mathbf{X}}_{t}\|^{2}-{\mathbb{E}}\left\{{\mathbb{I}}{\{a_{t}=i\}}\cdot({\mathbf{X}}_{t}^{\top}{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{(t)}-Y_{t})^{2}\ \|{\mathbf{X}}_{t}\|^{2}\right\}\right\}}_{=:\boldsymbol{\Xi}_{3}^{(t)}}.

Conditional on
{\mathcal{F}}_{t}
, it has

\displaystyle\|\boldsymbol{\Xi}_{1}^{(t)}|{\mathcal{F}}_{t}\|_{\Psi_{1}}\leq\lambda_{\max}\|{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{(t)}-{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{*}\|^{2},\quad\|\boldsymbol{\Xi}_{2}^{(t)}|{\mathcal{F}}_{t}\|_{\Psi_{1}}\leq\sigma\sqrt{\lambda_{\max}}\|{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{(t)}-{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{*}\|,

while under
{\mathcal{E}}_{t}^{X}:=\{\|{\mathbf{X}}_{t}\|^{2}\leq C\lambda_{\max}(d+\delta_{t})\}
,

\displaystyle\|\boldsymbol{\Xi}_{3}^{(t)}|{\mathcal{F}}_{t},\ {\mathcal{E}}_{t}\|_{\Psi_{1}}\leq C(d+\delta_{t})\lambda_{\max}\sigma^{2}+(d+\delta_{t})\lambda_{\max}^{2}\|{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{(t)}-{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{*}\|^{2}.

Specifically,
{\mathbb{P}}({\mathcal{E}}_{t}^{X}|{\mathcal{F}}_{t})\geq 1-\exp(-c_{2}(d+\delta_{t}))
.

THE FIRST PHASE
Define event

{\mathcal{E}}_{t}:=\left\{\|{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{(t)}-{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{*}\|^{2}\leq\frac{C^{*}\;(d+\delta_{t})}{t+C_{b}d}\frac{\sigma^{2}}{\lambda_{\min}},\quad i\in[K]\right\},

where
C^{*}
is some constant uncorrelated with
t,d,n
and will be specified later. We are going to prove by induction. It is obvious that
{\mathcal{E}}_{0}
holds. Then we shall show event
{\mathcal{E}}_{t+1}
holds under
\cup_{s=0}^{t}{\mathcal{E}}_{s}
. Substitue the exploration rate
\pi_{t}\geq\pi
into Proposition
3
and then it arrives at

{\mathbb{E}}\left\{\|{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{(t+1)}-{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{*}\|^{2}\big|{\mathcal{F}}_{t}\right\}\leq\|{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{(t)}-{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{*}\|^{2}-2\eta_{t}\frac{\pi}{K}\lambda_{\min}\|{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{(t)}-{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{*}\|^{2}+C\eta_{t}^{2}d\lambda_{\max}\sigma^{2}+C\eta_{t}^{2}d\lambda_{\max}^{2}\|{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{(t)}-{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{*}\|^{2}.

Then the estimation error dynamics have

\displaystyle\|{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{(t+1)}-{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{*}\|^{2}

\displaystyle={\mathbb{E}}\left\{\|{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{(t+1)}-{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{*}\|^{2}\big|{\mathcal{F}}_{t}\right\}+\|{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{(t+1)}-{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{*}\|^{2}-{\mathbb{E}}\left\{\|{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{(t+1)}-{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{*}\|^{2}\big|{\mathcal{F}}_{t}\right\}

\displaystyle\leq\|{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{(t)}-{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{*}\|^{2}-2\eta_{t}\frac{\pi}{K}\lambda_{\min}\|{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{(t)}-{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{*}\|^{2}+2\eta_{t}^{2}d\lambda_{\max}\sigma^{2}

\displaystyle~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~+2\eta_{t}^{2}d\lambda_{\max}^{2}\|{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{(t)}-{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}\|^{2}+2\eta_{t}\boldsymbol{\Xi}_{1}^{(t)}+2\eta_{t}\boldsymbol{\Xi}_{2}^{(t)}+\eta_{t}^{2}\boldsymbol{\Xi}_{3}^{(t)}.

Substitute the stepsize
\eta_{t}=\frac{1}{\lambda_{\min}}\frac{C_{a}}{t+C_{b}d}
into the above equation,

\|{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{(t+1)}-{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{*}\|^{2}\leq\left(1-\frac{\pi}{K}\frac{C_{a}}{t+C_{b}d}\right)\|{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{(t)}-{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{*}\|^{2}+\frac{2\,C_{a}^{2}}{(t+C_{b}d)^{2}}\,\frac{\lambda_{\max}}{\lambda_{\min}^{2}}\,d\,\sigma^{2}\\
-\frac{2\,C_{a}}{\lambda_{\min}}\frac{1}{t+C_{b}d}\,\boldsymbol{\Xi}_{1}^{(t)}-\frac{2\,C_{a}}{\lambda_{\min}}\frac{1}{t+C_{b}d}\,\boldsymbol{\Xi}_{2}^{(t)}+\frac{C_{a}^{2}}{\lambda_{\min}^{2}}\frac{1}{(t+C_{b}d)^{2}}\,\boldsymbol{\Xi}_{3}^{(t)},

where
2\eta_{t}d\leq\lambda_{\min}/\lambda_{\max}^{2}
is used. We accumulate the upper bound until
t=0
,

\displaystyle\|{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{(t+1)}-{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{*}\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\underbrace{\prod_{s=0}^{t}\left(1-\frac{\pi}{K}\frac{C_{a}}{s+C_{b}d}\right)\|{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{(0)}-{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{*}\|^{2}}_{\boldsymbol{\Delta}_{1}}+\underbrace{2\frac{\lambda_{\max}}{\lambda_{\min}^{2}}\sigma^{2}\sum_{s=0}^{t}\prod_{l=s+1}^{t}\left(1-\frac{\pi}{K}\frac{C_{a}}{l+C_{b}d}\right)\frac{C_{a}^{2}d}{(s+C_{b}d)^{2}}}_{\boldsymbol{\Delta}_{2}}

\displaystyle~~~~~~~~~~-\underbrace{\frac{2\,C_{a}}{\lambda_{\min}}\sum_{s=0}^{t}\prod_{l=s+1}^{t}\left(1-\frac{\pi}{K}\frac{C_{a}}{l+C_{b}d}\right)\frac{1}{s+C_{b}d}\boldsymbol{\Xi}_{1}^{(s)}}_{\boldsymbol{\Delta}_{3}}

\displaystyle~~~~~~~~~~-\underbrace{\frac{2\,C_{a}}{\lambda_{\min}}\sum_{s=0}^{t}\prod_{l=s+1}^{t}\left(1-\frac{\pi}{K}\frac{C_{a}}{l+C_{b}d}\right)\frac{1}{s+C_{b}d}\boldsymbol{\Xi}_{2}^{(s)}}_{\boldsymbol{\Delta}_{4}}

\displaystyle~~~~~~~~~~-\underbrace{\frac{C_{a}^{2}}{\lambda_{\min}^{2}}\sum_{s=0}^{t}\prod_{l=s+1}^{t}\left(1-\frac{\pi}{K}\frac{C_{a}}{l+C_{b}d}\right)\left(\frac{1}{s+C_{b}d}\right)^{2}\boldsymbol{\Xi}_{3}^{(s)}}_{\boldsymbol{\Delta}_{5}}.

Recall that we require
C_{a}\geq 2K/\pi
. Then, by Lemma
16
, we have

\displaystyle\prod_{s=0}^{t}\left(1-\frac{\pi}{K}\frac{C_{a}}{s+C_{b}d}\right)\leq\left(\frac{C_{b}\,d}{t+1+C_{b}d}\right)^{\frac{C_{a}\pi}{K}}\leq\frac{C_{b}\,d}{t+1+C_{b}d},

and

\displaystyle\left(1-\frac{\pi}{K}\frac{C_{a}}{s+1+C_{b}d}\right)\cdots\left(1-\frac{\pi}{K}\frac{C_{a}}{t+C_{b}d}\right)\left(\frac{1}{s+C_{b}d}\right)^{2}\leq\frac{\left(s+C_{b}d\right)^{\pi\cdot C_{a}/K-2}}{\left(t+1+C_{b}d\right)^{\pi\cdot C_{a}/K}}.

Then, sum up the above equation over
s
and follow Lemma
17
calculations, we have,

\displaystyle\sum_{s=0}^{t}\left(1-\frac{\pi}{K}\frac{C_{a}}{s+1+C_{b}d}\right)\cdots\left(1-\frac{\pi}{K}\frac{C_{a}}{t+C_{b}d}\right)\left(\frac{1}{s+C_{b}d}\right)^{2}\leq\frac{2K}{\pi\cdot C_{a}}\frac{1}{t+1+C_{b}d}.

Thus together with the initialization, we have the bound for
\boldsymbol{\Delta}_{1}
and
\boldsymbol{\Delta}_{2}
,

\boldsymbol{\Delta}_{1}\leq\frac{C_{b}\,d}{t+1+C_{b}d}\frac{\sigma^{2}}{\lambda_{\max}},\quad\boldsymbol{\Delta}_{2}\leq 4C_{a}\frac{K}{\pi}\frac{\lambda_{\max}}{\lambda_{\min}^{2}}\frac{d\,\sigma^{2}}{t+1+C_{b}d}.

Then consider
\boldsymbol{\Delta}_{3}
. The conditional Orlicz norm can be bounded with

\displaystyle\left\|\prod_{l=s+1}^{t}\left(1-\frac{\pi}{K}\frac{C_{a}}{l+C_{b}d}\right)\frac{1}{s+C_{b}d}\boldsymbol{\Xi}_{1}^{(s)}\bigg|{\mathcal{F}}_{s}\right\|_{\Psi_{1}}\leq C\lambda_{\max}\frac{\left(s+C_{b}d\right)^{C_{a}\cdot\pi/K-1}}{\left(t+1+C_{b}d\right)^{C_{a}\cdot\pi/K}}\cdot\|{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{(s)}-{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{*}\|^{2},

and under event
\cup_{s=0}^{t}{\mathcal{E}}_{s}
, Lemma
9
leads to

{\mathbb{P}}\left(\left|\sum_{s=0}^{t}\prod_{l=s+1}^{t}\left(1-\frac{\pi}{K}\frac{C_{a}}{l+C_{b}d}\right)\frac{1}{s+C_{b}d}\boldsymbol{\Xi}_{1}^{(s)}\right|\geq c_{1}\frac{C^{*}}{C_{a}}\frac{(d+\delta_{t+1})\ \sigma^{2}}{t+1+C_{b}d}\right)\\
\leq\exp\left(-c_{2}\frac{\pi}{K}\frac{C_{b}d+t}{C_{a}}\frac{\lambda_{\min}^{2}}{\lambda_{\max}^{2}}\right),

where
c_{1}<0.1
is some constant and under which we have

\displaystyle\big|\boldsymbol{\Delta}_{3}\big|\leq c_{1}C^{*}\frac{1}{\lambda_{\min}}\frac{(d+\delta_{t+1})\;\sigma^{2}}{t+1+C_{b}d}.

As for the term
\boldsymbol{\Delta}_{4}
, we have

\displaystyle\left\|\prod_{l=s+1}^{t}\left(1-\frac{\pi}{K}\frac{C_{a}}{l+C_{b}d}\right)\frac{1}{s+C_{b}d}\boldsymbol{\Xi}_{2}^{(s)}\bigg|{\mathcal{F}}_{s}\right\|_{\Psi_{1}}\leq C\sqrt{\lambda_{\max}}\sigma\frac{(s+C_{b}d)^{C_{a}\cdot\pi/K-1}}{(t+1+C_{b}d)^{C_{a}\cdot\pi/K}}\cdot\|{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{(s)}-{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{*}\|,

and then under event
\cup_{s=0}^{t}{\mathcal{E}}_{s}
, Lemma
9
guarantees

{\mathbb{P}}\left(\left|\sum_{s=0}^{t}\prod_{l=s+1}^{t}\left(1-\frac{\pi}{K}\frac{C_{a}}{l+C_{b}d}\right)\frac{1}{s+C_{b}d}\boldsymbol{\Xi}_{2}^{(s)}\right|\geq c_{1}\frac{C^{*}}{C_{a}}\frac{(d+\delta_{t+1})\ \sigma^{2}}{t+1+C_{b}d}\right)\\
\leq\exp\left(-c_{2}\min\left\{(d+\delta_{t+1})\frac{C^{*}}{C_{a}}\frac{\pi}{K}\frac{\lambda_{\min}}{\lambda_{\max}},\sqrt{d+\delta_{t+1}}\sqrt{C_{b}d+t}\frac{\sqrt{C^{*}}}{C_{a}}\frac{\sqrt{\lambda_{\min}}}{\sqrt{\lambda_{\max}}}\right\}\right).

Hence, we have the bound for
\boldsymbol{\Delta}_{4}
,

\displaystyle\big|\boldsymbol{\Delta}_{4}\big|\leq c_{1}\frac{C^{*}}{\lambda_{\min}}\frac{(d+\delta_{t+1})\;\sigma^{2}}{t+1+C_{b}d}.

Finally, under
{\mathcal{E}}_{s}^{X}
and
\|{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{t}-{\boldsymbol{\beta}}^{*}\|\leq 3\sigma^{2}/\lambda_{\max}
, the
\boldsymbol{\Delta}_{5}
term has

\left\|\prod_{l=s+1}^{t}\left(1-\frac{\pi}{K}\frac{C_{a}}{l+C_{b}d}\right)\left(\frac{1}{s+C_{b}d}\right)^{2}\boldsymbol{\Xi}_{3}^{(s)}\bigg|{\mathcal{F}}_{s},{\mathcal{E}}_{s}^{X}\right\|_{\Psi_{1}}\\
\leq C\lambda_{\max}(d+\delta_{s})\sigma^{2}\frac{\left(s+C_{b}d\right)^{C_{a}\cdot\pi/K-2}}{\left(t+1+C_{b}d\right)^{C_{a}\cdot\pi/K}}+C(d+\delta_{s})^{2}\lambda_{\max}^{2}\frac{(s+C_{b}s)^{C_{a}\cdot\pi/K-3}}{(t+1+C_{b}d)^{C_{a}\cdot\pi/K}}.

It implies that under event
\cup{\mathcal{E}}_{s}^{X}
, we have

{\mathbb{P}}\left(\left|\sum_{s=0}^{t}\prod_{l=s+1}^{t}\left(1-\frac{\pi}{K}\frac{C_{a}}{l+C_{b}d}\right)\left(\frac{1}{s+C_{b}d}\right)^{2}\boldsymbol{\Xi}_{3}^{(s)}\right|\geq c_{1}C^{*}\frac{\lambda_{\min}}{C_{a}^{2}}\frac{(d+\delta_{t+1})\ \sigma^{2}}{t+1+C_{b}d}\right)\\
\leq\exp\left(-c_{2}\left(d+\frac{t}{C_{b}}\right)\min\left\{\frac{\lambda_{\min}^{2}}{\lambda_{\max}^{2}}\frac{\pi}{K}\frac{C_{b}}{C_{a}}\left(\frac{C^{*}}{C_{a}}\right)^{2},\frac{\lambda_{\min}}{\lambda_{\max}}\frac{C^{*}C_{b}}{C_{a}^{2}}\right\}\right),

under which we have

\displaystyle\big|\boldsymbol{\Delta}_{5}\big|\leq c_{1}\frac{C^{*}}{\lambda_{\min}}\frac{(d+\delta_{t+1})\;\sigma^{2}}{t+1+C_{b}d}.

Thus, under the aboved mentioned events, we have

\displaystyle\|{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{(t+1)}-{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{*}\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{C_{b}\;d}{t+1+C_{b}d}\frac{\sigma^{2}}{\lambda_{\max}}+4C_{a}\frac{K}{\pi}\frac{\lambda_{\max}}{\lambda_{\min}^{2}}\frac{{\rm d}\sigma^{2}}{t+1+C_{b}d}+0.3\frac{C^{*}}{\lambda_{\min}}\frac{(d+\delta_{t+1})\;\sigma^{2}}{t+1+C_{b}d}.

With
C^{*}=8C_{a}\frac{K}{\pi}\frac{\lambda_{\max}}{\lambda_{\min}}+2C_{b}\frac{\lambda_{\min}}{\lambda_{\max}}
and
C_{b}\geq 3C_{a}\frac{K}{\pi}\frac{\lambda_{\max}^{2}}{\lambda_{\min}^{2}}
, we have

\|{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{(t+1)}-{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{*}\|^{2}\leq\frac{C^{*}\;(d+\delta_{t+1})}{t+1+C_{b}d}\frac{\sigma^{2}}{\lambda_{\min}},\quad\text{for all }i\in[K],

with probability excceeding
1-2K\exp\left(-cd-c\frac{t}{C_{a}}\right)-K\exp\left(-c(d+\delta_{t+1})\right)
.

THE SECOND PHASE
We will first consider the convergence dynamics of the set
{\mathcal{A}}
and then discuss
i\in{\mathcal{A}}^{c}
. In this phase,
\pi_{t}\in[0,1]
is arbitrary. We denote the event

\displaystyle{\mathcal{E}}_{t}:=\left\{\|{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{(t)}-{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{*}\|^{2}\leq\frac{C^{**}\,(d+\delta_{t})}{t+C_{b}d}\frac{\sigma^{2}}{\lambda_{\min}},\;i\in{\mathcal{A}},\quad\|{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{(t)}-{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{*}\|\leq\frac{h}{2},\;i\in{\mathcal{A}}^{c}\right\},

which is different from the event in the first phase. We prove by inducton. It is worth noting that
{\mathcal{E}}_{t_{1}}
holds. Then we shall prove under
\cup_{s=t_{1}}^{t}{\mathcal{E}}_{s}
,
{\mathcal{E}}_{t+1}
holds. Then given
\{\max_{i\in[K]}\|{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{(t)}-{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{*}\|\leq\frac{h}{2}\}
, Proposition
3
proves, for all
i\in{\mathcal{A}}
,

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}\left\{\|{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{(t+1)}-{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{*}\|^{2}\big|{\mathcal{F}}_{t}\right\}\leq\|{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{(t)}-{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{*}\|^{2}-\eta_{t}\lambda_{\min}\frac{1}{K}\|{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{(t)}-{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{*}\|^{2}+C\eta_{t}^{2}d\lambda_{\max}\sigma^{2},

where
\frac{\pi_{t}}{K}+(1-\pi_{t})\geq\frac{1}{K}
. Thus, the estimation error rates can be bounded with

\displaystyle\|{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{(t+1)}-{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{*}\|^{2}

\displaystyle={\mathbb{E}}\left\{\|{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{(t+1)}-{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{*}\|^{2}\big|{\mathcal{F}}_{t}\right\}+\left\{\|{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{(t+1)}-{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{*}\|^{2}-{\mathbb{E}}\left\{\|{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{(t+1)}-{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{*}\|^{2}\big|{\mathcal{F}}_{t}\right\}\right\}

\displaystyle\leq\|{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{(t)}-{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{*}\|^{2}-\eta_{t}\lambda_{\min}\frac{1}{K}\|{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{(t)}-{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{*}\|^{2}+C\eta_{t}^{2}d\lambda_{\max}\sigma^{2}-2\eta_{t}\boldsymbol{\Xi}_{1}^{(t)}-2\eta_{t}\boldsymbol{\Xi}_{2}^{(t)}+\eta_{t}^{2}\boldsymbol{\Xi}_{3}^{(t)}.

Insert the stepsize
\eta_{t}=\frac{C_{a}}{\lambda_{\min}}\ \frac{1}{t+C_{b}d}
into the above equation and then we have

\displaystyle\|{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{(t+1)}-{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{*}\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\left(1-\frac{C_{a}/K}{t+C_{b}d}\right)\|{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{(t)}-{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{*}\|^{2}+\frac{C\,C_{a}^{2}}{(t+C_{b}d)^{2}}\,\frac{\lambda_{\max}}{\lambda_{\min}^{2}}\,d\,\sigma^{2}

\displaystyle~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~-\frac{2\,C_{a}}{\lambda_{\min}}\frac{1}{t+C_{b}d}\,\boldsymbol{\Xi}_{1}^{(t)}-\frac{2\,C_{a}}{\lambda_{\min}}\frac{1}{t+C_{b}d}\,\boldsymbol{\Xi}_{2}^{(t)}+\frac{C_{a}^{2}}{\lambda_{\min}^{2}}\left(\frac{1}{t+C_{b}d}\right)^{2}\,\boldsymbol{\Xi}_{3}^{(t)}.

It leads to

\displaystyle\|{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{(t+1)}-{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{*}\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\underbrace{\prod_{s=t_{1}}^{t}\left(1-\frac{C_{a}/K}{s+C_{b}d}\right)\|{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{(t_{1})}-{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{*}\|^{2}}_{\boldsymbol{\Theta}_{1}}+\underbrace{C\frac{\lambda_{\max}}{\lambda_{\min}^{2}}\sigma^{2}\sum_{s=t_{1}}^{t}\prod_{l=s+1}^{t}\left(1-\frac{C_{a}/K}{l+C_{b}d}\right)\frac{C_{a}^{2}d}{(s+C_{b}d)^{2}}}_{\boldsymbol{\Theta}_{2}}

\displaystyle~~~~~~~~~~-\underbrace{\frac{2\,C_{a}}{\lambda_{\min}}\sum_{s=t_{1}}^{t}\prod_{l=s+1}^{t}\left(1-\frac{C_{a}/K}{l+C_{b}d}\right)\frac{1}{s+C_{b}d}\boldsymbol{\Xi}_{1}^{(s)}}_{\boldsymbol{\Theta}_{3}}

\displaystyle~~~~~~~~~~-\underbrace{\frac{2\,C_{a}}{\lambda_{\min}}\sum_{s=t_{1}}^{t}\prod_{l=s+1}^{t}\left(1-\frac{C_{a}/K}{l+C_{b}d}\right)\frac{1}{s+C_{b}d}\boldsymbol{\Xi}_{2}^{(s)}}_{\boldsymbol{\Theta}_{4}}

\displaystyle~~~~~~~~~~-\underbrace{\frac{C_{a}^{2}}{\lambda_{\min}^{2}}\sum_{s=t_{1}}^{t}\prod_{l=s+1}^{t}\left(1-\frac{C_{a}/K}{l+C_{b}d}\right)\left(\frac{1}{s+C_{b}d}\right)^{2}\boldsymbol{\Xi}_{3}^{(s)}}_{\boldsymbol{\Theta}_{5}}.

By Lemma
16
and
C_{a}>K
, we have

\displaystyle\prod_{s=t_{1}}^{t}\left(1-\frac{C_{a}/K}{s+C_{b}d}\right)\leq\frac{t_{1}+C_{b}d}{t+1+C_{b}d},

which proves
\boldsymbol{\Theta}_{1}\leq\frac{t_{1}+C_{b}d}{t+1+C_{b}d}\|{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{(t_{1})}-{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{*}\|^{2}\leq\frac{Cd}{t+1+C_{b}d}\frac{\sigma^{2}}{\lambda_{\min}}
. Moreover, Lemma
16
proves

\displaystyle\left(1-\frac{C_{a}/K}{s+1+C_{b}d}\right)\cdots\left(1-\frac{C_{a}/K}{t+C_{b}d}\right)\left(\frac{1}{s+C_{b}d}\right)^{2}\leq\left(\frac{s+C_{b}d}{t+1+C_{b}d}\right)^{C_{a}/K}\left(\frac{1}{s+C_{b}d}\right)^{2}.

Then, sum over
s
and Lemma
17
shows

\displaystyle\sum_{s=t_{1}}^{t}\left(1-\frac{C_{a}/K}{s+1+C_{b}d}\right)\cdots\left(1-\frac{C_{a}/K}{t+C_{b}d}\right)\left(\frac{1}{s+C_{b}d}\right)^{2}\leq\frac{2K}{C_{a}}\frac{1}{t+1+C_{b}d},

which proves.

\displaystyle\boldsymbol{\Theta}_{2}\leq C\frac{C_{a}}{\lambda_{\min}}\frac{\lambda_{\max}}{\lambda_{\min}}\frac{{\rm d}\sigma^{2}}{t+1+C_{b}d}.

Then consider the term
\boldsymbol{\Theta}_{3}
. Its single component has

\displaystyle\left\|\prod_{l=s+1}^{t}\left(1-\frac{C_{a}/K}{l+C_{b}d}\right)\frac{1}{s+C_{b}d}\boldsymbol{\Xi}_{1}^{(s)}\bigg|{\mathcal{F}}_{s}\right\|_{\Psi_{1}}\leq C\lambda_{\max}\frac{\left(s+C_{b}d\right)^{C_{a}/K-1}}{\left(t+1+C_{b}d\right)^{C_{a}/K}}\|{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{(s)}-{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{*}\|^{2}.

Under event
\cup_{s=t_{1}}^{t}{\mathcal{E}}_{s}
, Lemma
5
infers

\displaystyle{\mathbb{P}}\left(\left|\sum_{s=t_{1}}^{t}\prod_{l=s+1}^{t}\left(1-\frac{C_{a}/K}{l+C_{b}d}\right)\frac{1}{s+C_{b}d}\boldsymbol{\Xi}_{1}^{(s)}\right|\geq c_{1}\frac{C^{*}}{C_{a}}\frac{(d+\delta_{t+1})\ \sigma^{2}}{t+1+C_{b}d}\right)\leq\exp\left(-c_{2}(C_{b}d+t)\frac{1}{C_{a}}\frac{\lambda_{\min}^{2}}{\lambda_{\max}^{2}}\right),

where
c_{1}<0.1
is some small constant and then we have

\displaystyle\left|\boldsymbol{\Theta}_{3}\right|\leq c_{1}C^{**}\frac{1}{\lambda_{\min}}\frac{(d+\delta_{t+1})\;\sigma^{2}}{t+1+C_{b}d}.

As for the
\boldsymbol{\Theta}_{4}
term, we have

\displaystyle\left\|\prod_{l=s+1}^{t}\left(1-\frac{C_{a}/K}{l+C_{b}d}\right)\frac{1}{s+C_{b}d}\boldsymbol{\Xi}_{2}^{(s)}\bigg|{\mathcal{F}}_{s}\right\|_{\Psi_{1}}\leq C\sqrt{\lambda_{\max}}\sigma\frac{(s+C_{b}d)^{C_{a}/K-1}}{(t+1+C_{b}d)^{C_{a}/K}}\|{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{(s)}-{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{*}\|.

Then under event
\cup_{s=t_{1}}^{t}{\mathcal{E}}_{s}
, we have

{\mathbb{P}}\left(\left|\sum_{s=t_{1}}^{t}\prod_{l=s+1}^{t}\left(1-\frac{C_{a}/K}{l+C_{b}d}\right)\frac{1}{s+C_{b}d}\boldsymbol{\Xi}_{2}^{(s)}\right|\geq c_{1}\frac{C^{*}}{C_{a}}\frac{(d+\delta_{t+1})\ \sigma^{2}}{t+1+C_{b}d}\right)\\
\leq\exp\left(-c_{2}\min\left\{(d+\delta_{t+1})\frac{C^{*}}{C_{a}}\frac{\lambda_{\min}}{\lambda_{\max}},\sqrt{d+\delta_{t+1}}\sqrt{d+t/C_{b}}\frac{\sqrt{C^{*}C_{b}}}{C_{a}}\frac{\sqrt{\lambda_{\min}}}{\sqrt{\lambda_{\max}}}\right\}\right),

which leads to

\displaystyle\left|\boldsymbol{\Theta}_{4}\right|\leq c_{1}\frac{C^{**}}{\lambda_{\min}}\frac{(d+\delta_{t+1})\;\sigma^{2}}{t+1+C_{b}d}.

Finally, consider the term
\boldsymbol{\Theta}_{5}
. Under
{\mathcal{F}}_{s}
and under event
{\mathcal{E}}_{s},{\mathcal{E}}_{s}^{X}
, we have

\displaystyle\left\|\prod_{l=s+1}^{t}\left(1-\frac{C_{a}/K}{l+C_{b}d}\right)\left(\frac{1}{s+C_{b}d}\right)^{2}\boldsymbol{\Xi}_{3}^{(s)}\bigg|{\mathcal{F}}_{s},{\mathcal{E}}_{s}^{X}\right\|_{\Psi_{1}}\leq C\lambda_{\max}(d+\delta_{s})\sigma^{2}\frac{\left(s+C_{b}d\right)^{C_{a}-2}}{\left(t+1+C_{b}d\right)^{C_{a}}}.

Then under event
\cup_{s=t_{1}}^{t}\left\{{\mathcal{E}}_{s}\cup{\mathcal{E}}_{s}^{X}\right\}
, we have

{\mathbb{P}}\left(\left|\sum_{s=t_{1}}^{t}\prod_{l=s+1}^{t}\left(1-\frac{C_{a}/K}{l+C_{b}d}\right)\left(\frac{1}{s+C_{b}d}\right)^{2}\boldsymbol{\Xi}_{3}^{(s)}\right|\geq c_{1}C^{**}\frac{\lambda_{\min}}{C_{a}^{2}}\frac{(d+\delta_{t+1})\ \sigma^{2}}{t+1+C_{b}d}\right)\\
\leq\exp\left(-c_{2}\left(d+\frac{t}{C_{b}}\right)\min\left\{\frac{\lambda_{\min}^{2}}{\lambda_{\max}^{2}}\frac{C_{b}}{C_{a}}\left(\frac{C^{**}}{C_{a}}\right)^{2},\frac{\lambda_{\min}}{\lambda_{\max}}\frac{C^{**}C_{b}}{C_{a}^{2}}\right\}\right),

which shows

\displaystyle\left|\boldsymbol{\Theta}_{5}\right|\leq c_{1}\frac{C^{**}}{\lambda_{\min}}\frac{(d+\delta_{t+1})\;\sigma^{2}}{t+1+C_{b}d}.

Thus, altogether, with
C^{**}=C^{*}+2CC_{a}\frac{\lambda_{\max}}{\lambda_{\min}}
, we have

\displaystyle\|{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{(t+1)}-{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{*}\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{C^{*}}{\lambda_{\min}}\frac{d\,\sigma^{2}}{t+1+C_{b}d}+C\frac{C_{a}}{\lambda_{\min}}\frac{\lambda_{\max}}{\lambda_{\min}}\frac{{\rm d}\sigma^{2}}{t+1+C_{b}d}+3c_{1}\frac{C^{**}}{\lambda_{\min}}\frac{(d+\delta_{t+1})\;\sigma^{2}}{t+1+C_{b}d}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{C^{**}}{\lambda_{\min}}\frac{(d+\delta_{t+1})\;\sigma^{2}}{t+1+C_{b}d},

holds with probability exceeding
1-|{\mathcal{A}}|\exp(-c(d+t/C_{a}))-|{\mathcal{A}}|\exp(-c(d+\delta_{t+1}))
for all
i\in{\mathcal{A}}
. Moreover, with
C_{b}\geq C_{a}\frac{K}{\pi}\frac{\lambda_{\max}^{2}}{\lambda_{\min}^{2}}
, we have
\|{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{(t+1)}-{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{*}\|^{2}\leq C\frac{\sigma^{2}}{\lambda_{\max}}
; given
\frac{C^{*}}{\lambda_{\min}}\frac{(d+\delta_{t_{1}})\;\sigma^{2}}{t_{1}+C_{b}d}\leq\frac{h^{2}}{16}
, we have
\|{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{(t+1)}-{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{*}\|^{2}\leq\frac{h^{2}}{4}
.

Then consider bandits in
i\in{\mathcal{A}}^{c}
. Proposition
3
proves that under
{\mathcal{E}}_{t}
, we have

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}\left\{\|{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{(t+1)}-{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{*}\|^{2}\bigg|{\mathcal{F}}_{t}\right\}\leq\|{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{(t)}-{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{*}\|^{2}-2\eta_{t}\pi_{t}\lambda_{\min}\|{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{(t)}-{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{*}\|^{2}+C\eta_{t}^{2}\frac{\pi_{t}}{K}\lambda_{\max}\;d\sigma^{2}.

Substitute
\eta_{t}=\frac{C_{a}}{\lambda_{\min}}\frac{1}{t+C_{b}d}
into the above equation and then we have

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}\left\{\|{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{(t+1)}-{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{*}\|^{2}\bigg|{\mathcal{F}}_{t}\right\}\leq\left(1-\frac{C_{a}\,\pi_{t}}{t+C_{b}d}\right)\|{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{(t)}-{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{*}\|^{2}+CC_{a}^{2}\frac{\pi_{t}}{K}\frac{\lambda_{\max}}{\lambda_{\min}^{2}}\left(\frac{1}{t+C_{b}d}\right)^{2}.

Thus we have

\displaystyle\|{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{(t+1)}-{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{*}\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\underbrace{\prod_{s=t_{1}}^{t}\left(1-\frac{C_{a}\pi_{s}}{s+C_{b}d}\right)\|{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{(t_{1})}-{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{*}\|^{2}}_{\boldsymbol{\Gamma}_{1}}+\underbrace{CC_{a}^{2}d\sigma^{2}\frac{1}{K}\frac{\lambda_{\max}}{\lambda_{\min}^{2}}\sum_{s=t_{1}}^{t}\prod_{l=s+1}^{t}\left(1-\frac{C_{a}\pi_{l}}{l+C_{b}d}\right)\frac{\pi_{s}}{(s+C_{b}d)^{2}}}_{\boldsymbol{\Gamma}_{2}}

\displaystyle~~~~~~~~~~-\underbrace{\frac{2\,C_{a}}{\lambda_{\min}}\sum_{s=t_{1}}^{t}\prod_{l=s+1}^{t}\left(1-\frac{C_{a}\pi_{l}}{l+C_{b}d}\right)\frac{1}{s+C_{b}d}\boldsymbol{\Xi}_{1}^{(s)}}_{\boldsymbol{\Gamma}_{3}}

\displaystyle~~~~~~~~~~-\underbrace{\frac{2\,C_{a}}{\lambda_{\min}}\sum_{s=t_{1}}^{t}\prod_{l=s+1}^{t}\left(1-\frac{C_{a}\pi_{l}}{l+C_{b}d}\right)\frac{1}{s+C_{b}d}\boldsymbol{\Xi}_{2}^{(s)}}_{\boldsymbol{\Gamma}_{4}}

\displaystyle~~~~~~~~~~+\underbrace{\frac{C_{a}^{2}}{\lambda_{\min}^{2}}\sum_{s=t_{1}}^{t}\prod_{l=s+1}^{t}\left(1-\frac{C_{a}\pi_{l}}{l+C_{b}d}\right)\left(\frac{1}{s+C_{b}d}\right)^{2}\boldsymbol{\Xi}_{3}^{(s)}}_{\boldsymbol{\Gamma}_{5}}.

For
\boldsymbol{\Gamma}_{1}
, it simply has
\boldsymbol{\Gamma}_{1}\leq\|{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{(t_{1})}-{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{*}\|^{2}\leq\frac{h^{2}}{16}
. As for
\boldsymbol{\Gamma}_{2}
, we have

\displaystyle\boldsymbol{\Gamma}_{2}\leq CC_{a}^{2}d\sigma^{2}\frac{1}{K}\frac{\lambda_{\max}}{\lambda_{\min}^{2}}\sum_{s=t_{1}}^{t}\frac{1}{(s+C_{b}d)^{2}}\leq C\frac{C_{a}^{2}}{K}\frac{\lambda_{\max}}{\lambda_{\min}^{2}}\frac{d\sigma^{2}}{t_{1}+C_{b}d}.

It is worth noting that under event
\{\max_{j}\|{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{j}^{(s)}-{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{j}^{*}\|\leq\frac{h}{2}\}
,
a_{t}=i
only when
\alpha_{t}=1
and
{\mathbb{I}}\{a_{t}=i\}
is independent of
{\mathbf{X}}_{t}
,
{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{(t)}
, following
{\mathbb{I}}\{a_{t}=i\}\sim\operatorname*{Bernoulli}\left(\frac{\pi_{t}}{K}\right)
. Then a single component of
\boldsymbol{\Gamma}_{3}
has

\displaystyle\left\|\prod_{l=s+1}^{t}\left(1-\frac{C_{a}\pi_{l}}{l+C_{b}d}\right)\frac{1}{s+C_{b}d}\boldsymbol{\Xi}_{1}^{(s)}\bigg|{\mathcal{F}}_{s}\right\|_{\Psi_{1}}\leq{\mathbb{I}}\left\{\pi_{s}\neq 0\right\}\cdot\lambda_{\max}\frac{h^{2}}{s+C_{b}d}.

By Lemma
9
, we have

{\mathbb{P}}\left(\left|\sum_{s=t_{1}}^{t}\prod_{l=s+1}^{t}\left(1-\frac{C_{a}\pi_{l}}{l+C_{b}d}\right)\frac{1}{s+C_{b}d}\boldsymbol{\Xi}_{1}^{(s)}\right|>c_{1}{\mathbb{I}}\left\{\pi_{s}\neq 0\right\}h^{2}\frac{\lambda_{\min}}{C_{a}}\right)\\
\leq{\mathbb{I}}\left\{\pi_{s}\neq 0\right\}\exp\left(-c_{2}d\min\left\{\frac{C_{b}}{C_{a}^{2}}\frac{\lambda_{\min}^{2}}{\lambda_{\max}^{2}},\frac{C_{b}}{C_{a}}\frac{\lambda_{\min}}{\lambda_{\max}}\right\}\right),

under which it has

\displaystyle\big|\boldsymbol{\Gamma}_{3}\big|\leq c_{1}{\mathbb{I}}\left\{\pi_{s}\neq 0\right\}\cdot h^{2}.

Similarly, it has

\displaystyle\left\|\prod_{l=s+1}^{t}\left(1-\frac{C_{a}\pi_{l}}{l+C_{b}d}\right)\frac{1}{s+C_{b}d}\boldsymbol{\Xi}_{2}^{(s)}\bigg|{\mathcal{F}}_{s}\right\|_{\Psi_{1}}\leq{\mathbb{I}}\left\{\pi_{s}\neq 0\right\}\sqrt{\lambda_{\max}}\sigma\frac{h}{s+C_{b}d}.

Then Lemma
9
guarantees

{\mathbb{P}}\left(\left|\sum_{s=t_{1}}^{t}\prod_{l=s+1}^{t}\left(1-\frac{C_{a}\pi_{l}}{l+C_{b}d}\right)\frac{1}{s+C_{b}d}\boldsymbol{\Xi}_{2}^{(s)}\right|>c_{1}{\mathbb{I}}\left\{\pi_{s}\neq 0\right\}h^{2}\frac{\lambda_{\min}}{C_{a}}\right)\\
\leq{\mathbb{I}}\left\{\pi_{s}\neq 0\right\}\exp\left(-cd\min\left\{\frac{C^{*}}{C_{a}^{2}}\frac{\lambda_{\min}}{\lambda_{\max}},\frac{\sqrt{\lambda_{\min}}}{\sqrt{\lambda_{\max}}}\frac{\sqrt{C^{*}C_{b}}}{C_{a}}\right\}\right),

which implies
\big|\boldsymbol{\Gamma}_{4}\big|\leq c_{1}h^{2}
. Then consider the term
\boldsymbol{\Gamma}_{5}
,

\displaystyle\left\|\prod_{l=s+1}^{t}\left(1-\frac{C_{a}\pi_{l}}{l+C_{b}d}\right)\left(\frac{1}{s+C_{b}d}\right)^{2}\boldsymbol{\Xi}_{3}^{(s)}\bigg|{\mathcal{F}}_{s}\right\|_{\Psi_{1}}\leq{\mathbb{I}}\left\{\pi_{s}\neq 0\right\}\left(\frac{1}{s+C_{b}d}\right)^{2}d\lambda_{\max}\sigma^{2}.

Follow Lemma
9
,

{\mathbb{P}}\left(\left|\sum_{s=t_{1}}^{t}\prod_{l=s+1}^{t}\left(1-\frac{C_{a}\pi_{l}}{l+C_{b}d}\right)\left(\frac{1}{s+C_{b}d}\right)^{2}\boldsymbol{\Xi}_{3}^{(s)}\right|>c_{1}{\mathbb{I}}\left\{\pi_{s}\neq 0\right\}\frac{\lambda_{\min}^{2}}{C_{a}^{2}}h^{2}\right)\\
\leq{\mathbb{I}}\left\{\pi_{s}\neq 0\right\}\exp\left(-cd\min\left\{\frac{\lambda_{\min}^{2}}{\lambda_{\max}^{2}}\frac{C^{*2}C_{b}}{C_{a}^{4}},\frac{\lambda_{\min}}{\lambda_{\max}}\frac{C^{*}C_{b}}{C_{a}^{2}}\right\}\right),

by which we have
\big|\boldsymbol{\Gamma}_{5}\big|\leq c_{1}h^{2}
. Thus altogether, given
C_{b}\geq CC_{a}^{2}\lambda_{\max}^{2}/\lambda_{\min}^{2}
, the following holds for all
i\in{\mathcal{A}}^{c}
with probability over
1-{\mathbb{I}}\left\{\pi_{s}\neq 0\right\}|{\mathcal{A}}^{c}|\exp(-cd)

\displaystyle\|{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{(t+1)}-{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{*}\|^{2}\leq\frac{h^{2}}{4}.

∎

C.4
Proof of Theorem
5

Proof.

First, consider the regret at time
t
, which can be decomposed into terms,

{\mathbb{E}}\left\{\max_{i\in[K]}{\mathbf{X}}_{t}^{\top}{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{*}-{\mathbf{X}}_{t}^{\top}{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{a_{t}}^{*}\right\}\\
=\underbrace{{\mathbb{E}}\left\{{\mathbb{I}}\{\alpha_{t}=1\}\cdot\left(\max_{i\in[K]}{\mathbf{X}}_{t}^{\top}{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{*}-{\mathbf{X}}_{t}^{\top}{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{a_{t}}^{*}\right)\right\}}_{{\mathbf{R}}_{1}}+\underbrace{{\mathbb{E}}\left\{{\mathbb{I}}\{\alpha_{t}=0\}\cdot\left(\max_{i\in[K]}{\mathbf{X}}_{t}^{\top}{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{*}-{\mathbf{X}}_{t}^{\top}{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{a_{t}}^{*}\right)\right\}}_{{\mathbf{R}}_{2}}.

We shall analyze when
t\leq t_{1}
and
t>t_{1}
separately. When
t<t_{1}
,

\displaystyle{\mathbf{R}}_{1}

\displaystyle=\frac{\pi_{t}}{K}\cdot\sum_{j=1}^{K}{\mathbb{E}}\left\{\max_{i}{\mathbf{X}}_{t}^{\top}{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{*}-{\mathbf{X}}_{t}^{\top}{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{j}^{*}\right\}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\pi_{t}}{K}\cdot\min\left\{\sum_{j=1}^{K}\sum_{i=1}^{K}{\mathbb{E}}\left\{\left|{\mathbf{X}}_{t}^{\top}{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{*}-{\mathbf{X}}_{t}^{\top}{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{j}^{*}\right|\right\},\sum_{j=1}^{K}{\mathbb{E}}\left\{\|{\mathbf{X}}_{t}\|\cdot\max_{i}\|{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{*}-{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{j}^{*}\|\right\}\right\}

\displaystyle\leq\sqrt{\lambda_{\max}}\frac{\pi_{t}}{K}\cdot\min\left\{\sum_{j=1}^{K}\sum_{i=1}^{K}\|{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{*}-{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{j}^{*}\|,\sqrt{d}\sum_{j=1}^{K}\max_{i}\|{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{*}-{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{j}^{*}\|\right\}.

Denote event
{\mathcal{E}}_{t_{1}}:=\left\{\|{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{(t)}-{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{*}\|^{2}\leq C^{*}\frac{1}{\lambda_{\min}}\frac{d+\delta_{t}}{t+C_{b}d}\sigma^{2},\quad i\in[K],\text{ for all }t\in[t_{1}]\right\}
. Theorem
4
proves

{\mathbb{P}}({\mathcal{E}}_{t_{1}})\geq 1-c^{\prime}KC_{b}\exp(-cd)-K\exp(-cd)\sum_{s=0}^{t_{1}}\exp(-c\delta_{s}).

Denote event
{\mathcal{E}}^{(t)}:=\left\{\|{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{(t)}-{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{*}\|^{2}\leq C^{*}\frac{1}{\lambda_{\min}}\frac{d+\delta_{t}}{t+C_{b}d}\sigma^{2},\quad i\in[K]\right\}
. Then

{\mathbf{R}}_{2}=\underbrace{(1-\pi_{t}){\mathbb{E}}\left\{\left(\max_{i\in[K]}{\mathbf{X}}_{t}^{\top}{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{*}-{\mathbf{X}}_{t}^{\top}{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{a_{t}}^{*}\right)\cdot{\mathbb{I}}\left\{{\mathcal{E}}^{(t)}\right\}\right\}}_{{\mathbf{R}}_{21}}\\
+\underbrace{(1-\pi_{t}){\mathbb{E}}\left\{\left(\max_{i\in[K]}{\mathbf{X}}_{t}^{\top}{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{*}-{\mathbf{X}}_{t}^{\top}{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{a_{t}}^{*}\right)\cdot{\mathbb{I}}\{{\mathcal{E}}^{(t)C}\}\right\}}_{{\mathbf{R}}_{22}}.

Denote
a_{t}^{*}\in\arg\max_{i}{\mathbf{X}}^{\top}{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{*}
for simplicity. Observe that
{\mathbf{X}}_{t}^{\top}{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{a_{t}}^{(t)}\geq\max_{i}{\mathbf{X}}_{t}^{\top}{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{(t)}
and then
{\mathbf{R}}_{2}
has,

\displaystyle{\mathbf{R}}_{21}

\displaystyle\leq(1-\pi_{t})\cdot{\mathbb{E}}\left\{\left\{{\mathbf{X}}_{t}^{\top}{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{a_{t}^{*}}^{*}-{\mathbf{X}}_{t}^{\top}{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{a_{t}^{*}}^{(t)}+{\mathbf{X}}_{t}^{\top}{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{a_{t}}^{(t)}-{\mathbf{X}}_{t}^{\top}{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{a_{t}}^{*}\right\}\cdot{\mathbb{I}}\{{\mathcal{E}}^{(t)}\}\right\}

\displaystyle\leq(1-\pi_{t})\cdot{\mathbb{E}}\left\{\left|{\mathbf{X}}_{t}^{\top}{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{a_{t}^{*}}^{*}-{\mathbf{X}}_{t}^{\top}{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{a_{t}^{*}}^{(t)}\right|\cdot{\mathbb{I}}\{{\mathcal{E}}^{(t)}\}+\left|{\mathbf{X}}_{t}^{\top}{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{a_{t}}^{(t)}-{\mathbf{X}}_{t}^{\top}{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{a_{t}}^{*}\right|\cdot{\mathbb{I}}\{{\mathcal{E}}^{(t)}\}\right\}

\displaystyle\leq 2(1-\pi_{t})\cdot\min\left\{\sum_{i=1}^{K}{\mathbb{E}}\left|{\mathbf{X}}_{t}^{\top}\left({\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{*}-{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{(t)}\right)\right|\cdot{\mathbb{I}}\{{\mathcal{E}}^{(t)}\},{\mathbb{E}}\left|\|{\mathbf{X}}\|\cdot\max_{i}\|{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{(t)}-{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{*}\|\cdot{\mathbb{I}}\{{\mathcal{E}}^{(t)}\}\right|\right\}.

Thus taking event
{\mathcal{E}}^{(t)}
into consideration, we have

\displaystyle{\mathbf{R}}_{21}\leq 2(1-\pi_{t})\sqrt{C^{*}}\sqrt{\frac{\lambda_{\max}}{\lambda_{\min}}}\sqrt{\frac{d+\delta_{t}}{t+C_{b}d}}\,\sigma\,\min\left\{K,\;\sqrt{d}\right\}.

Similarly, we have

\displaystyle{\mathbf{R}}_{22}\leq(1-\pi_{t})\min\left\{\sum_{j=1}^{K}\sum_{i=1}^{K}{\mathbb{E}}\left|{\mathbf{X}}_{t}^{\top}\left({\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{*}-{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{j}^{*}\right)\right|,{\mathbb{E}}\|{\mathbf{X}}_{t}\|\cdot\max_{i,j}\|{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{*}-{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{j}^{*}\|\right\}\cdot{\mathbb{P}}({\mathcal{E}}^{(t)C}).

Also note that
\sum_{t=0}^{t_{1}}{\mathbb{P}}({\mathcal{E}}^{(t)C})=1-{\mathbb{P}}({\mathcal{E}}_{t_{1}})\leq c^{\prime}KC_{b}\exp(-cd)+K\exp(-cd)\sum_{s=0}^{t_{1}}\exp(-c\delta_{s})
. Thus, we have

\textsf{Regret}(t_{1})\leq\sqrt{\lambda_{\max}}\frac{\sum_{s=0}^{t_{1}}\pi_{s}}{K}\cdot\min\left\{\sum_{j=1}^{K}\sum_{i=1}^{K}\|{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{*}-{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{j}^{*}\|,\sqrt{d}\sum_{j=1}^{K}\max_{i}\|{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{*}-{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{j}^{*}\|\right\}\\
+C\sqrt{C^{*}}\sqrt{\frac{\lambda_{\max}}{\lambda_{\min}}}\sigma\min\left\{K,\sqrt{d}\right\}\sum_{s=0}^{t_{1}}\sqrt{\frac{d+\delta_{s}}{s+C_{b}d}}\\
+K\sqrt{\lambda_{\max}}\exp(-cd)\min\left\{\sum_{j=1}^{K}\sum_{i=1}^{K}{\mathbb{E}}\|{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{*}-{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{j}^{*}\|,\sqrt{d}\max_{i,j}\|{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{*}-{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{j}^{*}\|\right\}\cdot\left(c^{\prime}C_{b}+\sum_{s=0}^{t_{1}}\exp(-c\delta_{s})\right)

Then consider the regret for
t>t_{1}
. For
t>t_{1}
, the bound of
{\mathbf{R}}_{1}
is same as the case when
t\leq t_{1}
. For the analysis of
{\mathbf{R}}_{2}
, we denote

{\mathcal{E}}^{(t)}:=\left\{\|{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{(t)}-{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{*}\|^{2}\leq C^{*}\frac{1}{\lambda_{\min}}\frac{d+\delta_{t}}{t+C_{b}d}\sigma^{2},\ i\in{\mathcal{A}};\,\|{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{(t)}-{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{*}\|^{2}\leq\frac{h^{2}}{4},\ i\in{\mathcal{A}}^{c}\right\}.

Then according to Lemma
5
, we know under the above event,
a_{t}\in{\mathcal{A}}
. Thus, we have

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}\left\{\left(\max_{i\in[K]}{\mathbf{X}}_{t}^{\top}{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{*}-{\mathbf{X}}_{t}^{\top}{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{a_{t}}^{*}\right)\cdot{\mathbb{I}}\left\{{\mathcal{E}}^{(t)}\right\}\right\}

\displaystyle~~~~~\leq\min\left\{\sum_{i\in{\mathcal{A}}}{\mathbb{E}}\left|{\mathbf{X}}_{t}^{\top}\left({\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{*}-{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{(t)}\right)\right|\cdot{\mathbb{I}}\{{\mathcal{E}}^{(t)}\},{\mathbb{E}}\left|\|{\mathbf{X}}\|\cdot\max_{i\in{\mathcal{A}}}\|{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{(t)}-{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{*}\|\cdot{\mathbb{I}}\{{\mathcal{E}}^{(t)}\}\right|\right\}

\displaystyle~~~~~\leq\sqrt{C^{*}}\sqrt{\frac{\lambda_{\max}}{\lambda_{\min}}}\sqrt{\frac{d+\delta_{t}}{t+C_{b}d}}\,\sigma\,\min\left\{K,\;\sqrt{d}\right\}.

The analysis of
{\mathbb{E}}\left\{\left(\max_{i\in[K]}{\mathbf{X}}_{t}^{\top}{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{*}-{\mathbf{X}}_{t}^{\top}{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{a_{t}}^{*}\right)\cdot{\mathbb{I}}\left\{{\mathcal{E}}^{(t)C}\right\}\right\}
is same as the
t<t_{1}
case. Thus in all, we have

\textsf{Regret}(T)\leq\sqrt{\lambda_{\max}}\frac{\sum_{s=0}^{T}\pi_{s}}{K}\cdot\min\left\{\sum_{j=1}^{K}\sum_{i=1}^{K}\|{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{*}-{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{j}^{*}\|,\sqrt{d}\sum_{j=1}^{K}\max_{i}\|{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{*}-{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{j}^{*}\|\right\}\\
+C\sqrt{C^{*}}\sqrt{\frac{\lambda_{\max}}{\lambda_{\min}}}\sigma\min\left\{K,\sqrt{d}\right\}\sum_{s=0}^{T}\sqrt{\frac{d+\delta_{s}}{s+C_{b}d}}\\
+\sqrt{\lambda_{\max}}\min\left\{\sum_{j=1}^{K}\sum_{i=1}^{K}{\mathbb{E}}\|{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{*}-{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{j}^{*}\|,\sqrt{d}\max_{i,j}\|{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{*}-{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{j}^{*}\|\right\}\\
\times\left(c^{\prime}C_{b}\exp(-cd)+K\sum_{s=0}^{t_{1}}\exp(-c(\delta_{s}+d))+|{\mathcal{A}}|\sum_{s=t_{1}+1}^{T}\exp(-c(\delta_{s}+d))+|{\mathcal{A}}^{c}|\sum_{s=t_{1}+1}^{T}{\mathbb{I}}\{\pi_{s}\neq 0\}\right),

which completes the proof.
∎

C.5
Proof of Proposition
2

For brevity of writing, we introduce some notations. We denote

\displaystyle{\boldsymbol{\Sigma}}_{i,t}

\displaystyle:={\mathbb{E}}\left\{{\mathbb{I}}\{a_{t}=i\}\cdot{\mathbf{X}}_{t}{\mathbf{X}}_{t}^{\top}\bigg|{\mathcal{F}}_{t}\right\}

\displaystyle=\frac{\pi_{t}}{K}{\mathbb{E}}\left\{{\mathbf{X}}_{t}{\mathbf{X}}_{t}^{\top}\big|{\mathcal{F}}_{t}\right\}+(1-\pi_{t})\cdot{\mathbb{E}}\left\{{\mathbf{X}}_{t}{\mathbf{X}}_{t}^{\top}\cdot{\mathbb{I}}\left\{{\mathbf{X}}_{t}\in{\mathcal{X}}\left(i,\left\{{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{j}^{(t)}\right\}_{i\in[K]}\right)\right\}\bigg|{\mathcal{F}}_{t}\right\},

and

\displaystyle{\boldsymbol{\Sigma}}_{i,t}^{*}:=\frac{\pi_{t}}{K}{\mathbb{E}}\left\{{\mathbf{X}}_{t}{\mathbf{X}}_{t}^{\top}\big|{\mathcal{F}}_{t}\right\}+(1-\pi_{t})\cdot{\mathbb{E}}\left\{{\mathbf{X}}_{t}{\mathbf{X}}_{t}^{\top}\cdot{\mathbb{I}}\left\{{\mathbf{X}}_{t}\in{\mathcal{U}}_{i}^{*}\right\}\big|{\mathcal{F}}_{t}\right\}.

The matrix
{\boldsymbol{\Sigma}}_{i,t}^{*}
satisfies the following equation

\displaystyle\left(\frac{\pi_{t}}{K}+(1-\pi_{t})\right)\cdot\lambda_{\min}\cdot{\mathbf{I}}_{d}\preceq{\boldsymbol{\Sigma}}_{i,t}^{*}\preceq\lambda_{\max}\cdot{\mathbf{I}}_{d},

(23)

and we have the convergence in mean that

\displaystyle\lim_{t\to+\infty}{\mathbb{E}}\left\|{\boldsymbol{\Sigma}}_{i,t}^{*}-{\boldsymbol{\Sigma}}_{i}(\pi^{*})\right\|=0.

(24)

Additionally,
{\boldsymbol{\Sigma}}_{i,t}-{\boldsymbol{\Sigma}}_{i,t}^{*}
can be written into,

\left\|{\boldsymbol{\Sigma}}_{i,t}-{\boldsymbol{\Sigma}}_{i,t}^{*}\right\|=(1-\pi_{t})\left\|{\mathbb{E}}\left\{{\mathbf{X}}_{t}{\mathbf{X}}_{t}^{\top}\cdot\left({\mathbb{I}}\left\{{\mathbf{X}}\in{\mathcal{X}}_{t}\left(i,\left\{{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{j}^{(t)}\right\}_{i\in[K]}\right)\right\}-{\mathbb{I}}\left\{{\mathbf{X}}_{t}\in{\mathcal{U}}_{i}^{*}\right\}\right)\bigg|{\mathcal{F}}_{t}\right\}\right\|

Under Assumption
7
and Lemma
4
,
\left\|{\boldsymbol{\Sigma}}_{i,t}-{\boldsymbol{\Sigma}}_{i,t}^{*}\right\|
can be upper bounded with,

\displaystyle\left\|{\boldsymbol{\Sigma}}_{i,t}-{\boldsymbol{\Sigma}}_{i,t}^{*}\right\|\leq\left\{\begin{array}[]{ll}(1-\pi_{t})\cdot\kappa_{0}\cdot\max_{i\in{\mathcal{A}}}\|{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{(t)}-{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{*}\|,&\quad\max_{i\in[K]}\|{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{(t)}-{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{*}\|\leq h_{0}\\
(1-\pi_{t})\cdot\lambda_{\max},&\quad\text{for all }\{{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}\}_{i\in[K]}\end{array}.\right.

We first prove the following proposition.

Proposition 4
.

Under the same assumptions and conditions as Proposition
2
, we have

(1)

if
\pi^{*}=0
, then under the event
\cup_{t=t_{1}}\{\max\|{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{(t)}-{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{*}\|\leq\frac{h}{2}\}
, the following holds for
i\in{\mathcal{A}}
,

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}\|{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{(t)}-{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{*}\|^{2}\leq C\frac{d}{t+C_{b}d}\frac{\sigma^{2}}{\lambda_{\min}},

(2)

if
\pi^{*}>0
and
C_{a}\geq 4K/\pi^{*}
, the following holds for all
i\in[K]
,

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}\|{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{(t+1)}-{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{*}\|^{2}\leq\left(\frac{N+1+C_{b}d}{t+1+C_{b}d}\right)^{C_{a}\pi^{*}/K}\|{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{(N)}-{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{*}\|^{2}+\frac{Cd}{t+1+C_{b}d}\frac{\sigma^{2}}{\lambda_{\min}},

where
N
is some integer such that for all
t\geq N
,
\pi_{t}\geq\pi^{*}/2
.

Proof.

We first prove the
\pi^{*}=0
case and then we discuss the
\pi^{*}>0
scheme.

When
\pi^{*}=0
.
For
i\in{\mathcal{A}}
, Proposition
3
proves that

{\mathbb{E}}\left\{\|{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{(t+1)}-{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{*}\|^{2}\big|{\mathcal{F}}_{t}\right\}\leq\|{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{(t)}-{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{*}\|^{2}-2\eta_{t}\frac{\pi_{t}}{K}\lambda_{\min}\|{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{(t)}-{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{*}\|^{2}+2\eta_{t}^{2}d\lambda_{\max}^{2}\|{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{(t)}-{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{*}\|^{2}\\
-2\eta_{t}(1-\pi_{t})\lambda_{\min}\|{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{(t)}-{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{*}\|^{2}\cdot{\mathbb{I}}\left\{\max_{j}\|{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{j}^{(t)}-{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{*}\|\leq\frac{h}{2}\right\}+2\eta_{t}^{2}d\lambda_{\max}\sigma^{2}.

Take expectation on each side of the above equation and it leads to

{\mathbb{E}}\left\{\|{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{(t+1)}-{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{*}\|^{2}\right\}\leq\left(1-2\eta_{t}\frac{\pi_{t}}{K}\lambda_{\min}+2\eta_{t}^{2}d\lambda_{\max}^{2}\right){\mathbb{E}}\left\{\|{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{(t)}-{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{*}\|^{2}\right\}\\
-2\eta_{t}(1-\pi_{t})\lambda_{\min}{\mathbb{E}}\left\{\|{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{(t)}-{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{*}\|^{2}\cdot{\mathbb{I}}\left\{\max_{j}\|{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{j}^{(t)}-{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{*}\|\leq\frac{h}{2}\right\}\right\}+2\eta_{t}^{2}d\lambda_{\max}\sigma^{2}.

For
t\leq t_{1}
, according to the exploration rate scheme we have
\pi_{t}\geq\pi
.
Hence, we have

{\mathbb{E}}\left\{\|{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{(t+1)}-{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{*}\|^{2}\right\}\leq\left(1-2\frac{C_{a}}{t+C_{b}d}\frac{\pi}{K}+2\frac{C_{a}^{2}d}{(t+C_{b}d)^{2}}\frac{\lambda_{\max}^{2}}{\lambda_{\min}^{2}}\right){\mathbb{E}}\left\{\|{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{(t)}-{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{*}\|^{2}\right\}\\
+2\frac{C_{a}^{2}d}{(t+C_{b}d)^{2}}\frac{\lambda_{\max}}{\lambda_{\min}^{2}}\sigma^{2}.

According to the choice of
C_{b}
, we have

\displaystyle 2\frac{C_{a}^{2}d}{(t+C_{b}d)^{2}}\frac{\lambda_{\max}^{2}}{\lambda_{\min}^{2}}\leq\frac{C_{a}}{t+C_{b}d}\frac{\pi}{K}.

Hence, we have

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}\left\{\|{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{(t+1)}-{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{*}\|^{2}\right\}\leq\left(1-2\frac{C_{a}}{t+C_{b}d}\frac{\pi}{K}\right){\mathbb{E}}\left\{\|{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{(t)}-{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{*}\|^{2}\right\}+2\frac{C_{a}^{2}d}{(t+C_{b}d)^{2}}\frac{\lambda_{\max}}{\lambda_{\min}^{2}}\sigma^{2}

\displaystyle~~~\leq\prod_{l=0}^{t}\left(1-2\frac{C_{a}}{l+C_{b}d}\frac{\pi}{K}\right){\mathbb{E}}\left\{\|{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{(0)}-{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{*}\|^{2}\right\}+2\sum_{k=0}^{t}\prod_{l=k}^{t}\left(1-2\frac{C_{a}}{l+C_{b}d}\frac{\pi}{K}\right)\frac{C_{a}^{2}d}{(k+C_{b}d)^{2}}\frac{\lambda_{\max}}{\lambda_{\min}^{2}}\sigma^{2}.

By Lemma
16
and Lemma
17
, we have

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}\left\{\|{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{(t+1)}-{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{*}\|^{2}\right\}\leq\frac{Cd}{t+1+C_{b}d}\frac{\sigma^{2}}{\lambda_{\min}}.

On the other hand, for
t\geq t_{1}
, under the conditions
\cup_{t=t_{1}}^{+\infty}\{\max_{j\in[k]}\|{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{j}^{(t)}-{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{j}^{*}\|\leq\frac{h}{2}\}
, we have

{\mathbb{E}}\left\{\|{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{(t+1)}-{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{*}\|^{2}\right\}\leq\left(1-2\frac{C_{a}}{t+C_{b}d}\frac{1}{K}+2\frac{C_{a}^{2}d}{(t+C_{b}d)^{2}}\frac{\lambda_{\max}^{2}}{\lambda_{\min}^{2}}\right){\mathbb{E}}\left\{\|{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{(t)}-{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{*}\|^{2}\right\}\\
+2\frac{C_{a}^{2}d}{(t+C_{b}d)^{2}}\frac{\lambda_{\max}}{\lambda_{\min}^{2}}\sigma^{2}.

In a similar way, we have
{\mathbb{E}}\left\{\|{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{(t+1)}-{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{*}\|^{2}\right\}\leq\frac{Cd}{t+1+C_{b}d}\frac{\sigma^{2}}{\lambda_{\min}}
.

When
\pi^{*}>0
.
For all
i\in[K]
, we have

{\mathbb{E}}\left\{\|{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{(t+1)}-{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{*}\|^{2}\right\}\leq\left(1-2\frac{C_{a}}{t+C_{b}d}\frac{\pi_{t}}{K}+2\frac{C_{a}^{2}d}{(t+C_{b}d)^{2}}\frac{\lambda_{\max}^{2}}{\lambda_{\min}^{2}}\right){\mathbb{E}}\left\{\|{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{(t)}-{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{*}\|^{2}\right\}\\
+2\frac{C_{a}^{2}d}{(t+C_{b}d)^{2}}\frac{\lambda_{\max}}{\lambda_{\min}^{2}}\sigma^{2}.

In this case,
\lim_{t\to+\infty}\pi_{t}=\pi^{*}
and there exists
N>0
such that for all
t\geq N
, we have
\pi_{t}>\frac{1}{2}\pi^{*}
. Then by accumulating the above inequality, we have

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}\left\{\|{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{(t+1)}-{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{*}\|^{2}\right\}

\displaystyle\leq\prod_{l=N}^{t}\left(1-\frac{C_{a}}{l+C_{b}d}\frac{\pi^{*}}{K}\right){\mathbb{E}}\left\{\|{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{(N)}-{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{*}\|^{2}\right\}

\displaystyle~~~~~~~~+2\sum_{k=N}^{t}\prod_{l=k}^{t}\left(1-\frac{C_{a}}{l+C_{b}d}\frac{\pi^{*}}{K}\right)\frac{C_{a}^{2}d}{(k+C_{b}d)^{2}}\frac{\lambda_{\max}}{\lambda_{\min}^{2}}\sigma^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\left(\frac{N+1+C_{b}d}{t+1+C_{b}d}\right)^{C_{a}\pi^{*}/K}\|{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{(N)}-{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{*}\|^{2}+\frac{Cd}{t+1+C_{b}d}\frac{\sigma^{2}}{\lambda_{\min}},

where the last line uses Lemma
17
and
16
.

∎

Proposition 5
.

Under the same assumptions and conditions as Proposition
2
, we have

(1)

if
\pi^{*}=0
, then under the event
\cup_{t=t_{1}}\{\max\|{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{(t)}-{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{*}\|\leq\frac{h}{2}\}
, the following holds for
i\in{\mathcal{A}}
,

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}\|{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{(t)}-{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{*}\|^{4}\leq C\left(\frac{d}{t+C_{b}d}\right)^{2}\frac{\sigma^{4}}{\lambda_{\min}^{2}},

(2)

if
\pi^{*}>0
and
C_{a}\geq 4K/\pi^{*}
, the following holds for all
i\in[K]
,

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}\|{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{(t+1)}-{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{*}\|^{4}\leq\left(\frac{N+1+C_{b}d}{t+1+C_{b}d}\right)^{2C_{a}\pi^{*}/K}\|{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{N}-{\boldsymbol{\beta}}^{*}\|^{4}+C\left(\frac{d}{t+1+C_{b}d}\right)^{2}\frac{\sigma^{4}}{\lambda_{\min}^{2}},

where
N
is some integer such that for all
t\geq N
,
\pi_{t}\geq\pi^{*}/2
.

To prove Proposition
5
, we need to expand the fourth moment based on the update of
{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{(t+1)}
. The proof of Proposition
5
is similar to Proposition
4
, hence omitted.

Proof of Proposition
2
.

Under the conditions of Proposition
2
, Proposition
4
proves

\|{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{(t)}-{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{*}\|^{2}=O_{{\mathbb{P}}}\left(\frac{1}{t}\right).

Recall that the update is,

\displaystyle{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{(t+1)}-{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{*}

\displaystyle={\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{(t)}-{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{*}-\eta_{t}\cdot{\mathbb{I}}\{a_{t}=i\}\cdot({\mathbf{X}}_{t}^{\top}{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{(t)}-Y_{t}){\mathbf{X}}_{t}

\displaystyle=\left({\mathbf{I}}-\eta_{t}\cdot{\mathbb{I}}\{a_{t}=i\}\cdot{\mathbf{X}}_{t}{\mathbf{X}}_{t}^{\top}\right)\left({\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{(t)}-{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{*}\right)+\eta_{t}\cdot{\mathbb{I}}\{a_{t}=i\}\cdot\xi_{t}\cdot{\mathbf{X}}_{t},

which can be decomposed into,

\displaystyle{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{(t+1)}-{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{*}

\displaystyle=\left({\mathbf{I}}-\eta_{t}\cdot{\mathbb{E}}\left\{{\mathbb{I}}\{a_{t}=i\}\cdot{\mathbf{X}}_{t}{\mathbf{X}}_{t}^{\top}\bigg|{\mathcal{F}}_{t}\right\}\right)\left({\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{(t)}-{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{*}\right)

\displaystyle-\eta_{t}\cdot\left({\mathbb{I}}\{a_{t}=i\}\cdot{\mathbf{X}}_{t}{\mathbf{X}}_{t}^{\top}-{\mathbb{E}}\left\{{\mathbb{I}}\{a_{t}=i\}\cdot{\mathbf{X}}_{t}{\mathbf{X}}_{t}^{\top}\big|{\mathcal{F}}_{t}\right\}\right)\left({\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{(t)}-{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{*}\right)

\displaystyle+\eta_{t}\cdot{\mathbf{X}}_{t}\cdot{\mathbb{I}}\{a_{t}=i\}\cdot\xi_{t}.

With the notations defined above, the update can be further written as,

{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{(t+1)}-{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{*}=\left({\mathbf{I}}-\eta_{t}\cdot{\boldsymbol{\Sigma}}_{i,t}^{*}\right)\left({\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{(t)}-{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{*}\right)+\eta_{t}\cdot{\mathbb{I}}\{a_{t}=i\}\cdot\xi_{t}\cdot{\mathbf{X}}_{t}\\
-\eta_{t}\left({\boldsymbol{\Sigma}}_{i,t}-{\boldsymbol{\Sigma}}_{i,t}^{*}\right)\left({\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{(t)}-{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{*}\right)-\eta_{t}\cdot\left({\mathbb{I}}\{a_{t}=i\}\cdot{\mathbf{X}}_{t}{\mathbf{X}}_{t}^{\top}-{\boldsymbol{\Sigma}}_{i,t}\right)\left({\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{(t)}-{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{*}\right).

We accumulate the above update starting from
{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{i}^{(0)}
and it arrives at
